<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--daa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰दा</span> <i>-dā</i> fois (forme des adv. de temps).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृढ</span> <i>dṛḍha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-d_rh" class="skt-link">dṛh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_r_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛḍhā</i> ferme, fixe; stable, constant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> solide, massif; dur, dense <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fort, résistant; tenace, inébranlable.<br> <i>dṛḍhībhū</i> devenir fort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_dhabala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛḍhabala</i> [<a href="#entry-bala" class="skt-link">bala</a>] <a href="#entry-d_r_dhabala" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du médecin Dṛḍhabala; il révisa la Carakasaṃhitā.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_dhabhakti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

</div>

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_dhadasyu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛḍhadasyu</i> var. <i>dṛḍhasyu</i> [<a href="#entry-dasyu" class="skt-link">dasyu</a>] <a href="#entry-d_r_dhadasyu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du sage Dṛḍhadasyu, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uagasti" class="skt-link">Agasti</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ulopaamudraa" class="skt-link">Lopāmudrā</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uidhmavaaha" class="skt-link">Idhmavāha</a> ou <a href="#entry-Utridasyu" class="skt-link">Tridasyu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛḍhamati</i> [<a href="#entry-mati" class="skt-link">mati</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_r_dhamati" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_dhamati" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_dhamati" class="skt-link">f.</a> ferme d'esprit; engagé, résolu.
  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_dhasyu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृढस्यु</span> <i>dṛḍhasyu</i> cf. <a href="#entry-d_r_dhadasyu" class="skt-link">dṛḍhadasyu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_dhatara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛḍhatara</i> [<a href="#entry--tara" class="skt-link">-tara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_r_dhatara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_dhatara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_dhataraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛḍhatarā</i> compar. très fort; très violent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_dhavrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛḍhavrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_r_dhavrata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_dhavrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_dhavrataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛḍhavratā</i> [«ferme dans ses vœux»] qui suit ses résolutions; qui est ferme dans son ascèse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> voué à, qui persévère dans ⟨iic. loc.⟩.<br> <a href="#entry-d_r_dha" class="skt-link">dṛḍha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_mh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृंह्</span> <i>dṛṃh</i> cf. <a href="#entry-d_rh" class="skt-link">dṛh</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृष्ट</span> <i>dṛṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛṣṭā</i> vu, observé, remarqué, perçu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> visible, apparent, manifeste, réel, évident <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éprouvé, compris; reconnu, valable.<br> <i>dṛṣṭavīryo</i> <i>me</i> <i>rāmaḥ</i> <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> a été témoin de mes exploits.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_s_taad_r_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛṣṭādṛṣṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-ad_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">adṛṣṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_r_s_taad_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_taad_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_taad_r_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛṣṭādṛṣṭā</i> plus ou moins visible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_s_taanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛṣṭānta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] <a href="#entry-d_r_s_taanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> exemple; instance; illustration <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> analogie, parabole, métaphore <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] exemple à l'appui du syllogisme [<a href="#entry-pa_ncaavayava" class="skt-link">pañcāvayava</a>]; il doit vérifier la thèse [<a href="#entry-sapak_sa" class="skt-link">sapakṣa</a>].<br> <i>hetoḥ</i> <i>pratibandhāvadhāraṇaṃ</i> <i>dṛṣṭānta</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-Unyaayama_njarii" class="skt-link">Nyāyamañjarī</a>] L'exemple à l'appui est l'affirmation de l'implication de la cause.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_s_taantatas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛṣṭāntatas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. par exemple.<br> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_taanta" class="skt-link">dṛṣṭānta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_s_taartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛṣṭārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_r_s_taartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_taartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_taarthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛṣṭārthā</i> dont le mérite, le sens ou le but est évident <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui connaît le vrai sens des choses <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. qui concerne le monde visible; syn. <a href="#entry-laukika" class="skt-link">laukika</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">dṛṣṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_s_tapuurva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛṣṭapūrva</i> [<a href="#entry-puurva" class="skt-link">pūrva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_r_s_tapuurva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_tapuurva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_tapuurvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛṣṭapūrvā</i> déjà vu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृष्टि</span> <i>dṛṣṭi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] <a href="#entry-d_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> vue, vision; regard, coup d'œil, œil; notion, point de vue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> compréhension, intelligence; connaissance.<br> <i>dṛṣṭiṃ</i> <i>dā</i> regarder ⟨loc.⟩; jeter un coup d'œil.<br> <i>mama</i> <i>dṛṣṭyā</i> à mon avis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_s_tik_sama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_s_timanohara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

</div>

</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_s_tivi_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛṣṭiviṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-vi_sa" class="skt-link">viṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_r_s_tivi_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_tivi_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_s_tivi_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛṣṭiviṣā</i> myth. [«au regard empoisonné»] qui empoisonne par le simple regard.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृषद्</span> <i>dṛṣad</i> <a href="#entry-d_r_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> pierre, rocher; meule (inférieure, portant la pierre [<a href="#entry-upalaa" class="skt-link">upalā</a>]); syn. <a href="#entry-zilaa" class="skt-link">śilā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_sadupala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛṣadupala</i> [<a href="#entry-upala" class="skt-link">upala</a>] <a href="#entry-d_r_sadupala" class="skt-link">n.</a> meule à condiments — <a href="#entry-d_r_sadupalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛṣadupalā</i> du. <i>dṛṣadupale</i> les deux pierres de la meule.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_sadvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛṣadvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_r_sadvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_sadvat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_r_sadvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛṣadvatī</i> plein de rochers — <a href="#entry-d_r_sadvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛṣadvatī</i> géo. np. de la rivière Dṛṣadvatī «Qui charrie des pierres» (mod. Ghaggar, à la frontière entre le Pañjab et l'<a href="#entry-Uhariyaa_na" class="skt-link">Hariyāṇa</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-d_r_sad" class="skt-link">dṛṣad</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृ</span> <i>dṛ_1</i> ps. <i>(driyate)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>dṛtya</i>, <i>daraṇīya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_r_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृ</span> <i>dṛ_2</i> cf. <a href="#entry-d_rr" class="skt-link">dṝ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rbdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृब्ध</span> <i>dṛbdha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-d_rbh" class="skt-link">dṛbh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_rbdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_rbdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_rbdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛbdhā</i> lié, attaché; en bottes, en gerbes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rbh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृभ्</span> <i>dṛbh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(dṛbhati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dṛbdha</i><i>)</i> ca. <i>(darbhayati)</i> mettre en gerbes, lier, attacher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृह्</span> <i>dṛh</i> var. <i>dṛṃh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(dṛṃhati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dṛḍha</i><i>)</i> consolider, affermir — pr. md. <i>(dṛṃhate)</i> être ferme, être fort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rk">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृक्</span> <i>dṛk</i> cf. <a href="#entry-d_rz_2" class="skt-link">dṛś_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rkpaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛkpāta</i> [<a href="#entry-paata" class="skt-link">pāta</a>] <a href="#entry-d_rkpaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de jeter un regard.<br> <a href="#entry-d_rk" class="skt-link">dṛk</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rp">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृप्</span> <i>dṛp</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(dṛpyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dṛpta</i><i>)</i> devenir fou, divaguer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'enorgueillir, être orgueilleux — ca. <i>(darpayati)</i> affoler, rendre orgueilleux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rpta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृप्त</span> <i>dṛpta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-d_rp" class="skt-link">dṛp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_rpta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_rpta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_rptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛptā</i> fou, frénétique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> orgueilleux, arrogant, présomptueux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दॄ</span> <i>dṝ</i> var. <i>dṛ_2</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(dṛṇāti)</i> ca. <i>(dārayati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> fendre, déchirer, briser — ps. <i>(dīryate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dīrṇa</i><i>)</i> éclater, se fendre, se déchirer, tomber en pièces <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. δερω; ang. to tear; all. verzehren.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृति</span> <i>dṛti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-d_rr" class="skt-link">dṝ</a>] <a href="#entry-d_rti" class="skt-link">m.</a> peau (tannée), cuir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sac de cuir; outre en peau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rtihari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dṛtihari</i> [<a href="#entry-hari_1" class="skt-link">hari_1</a>] <a href="#entry-d_rtihari" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui transporte des charognes»] chien.<br> <a href="#entry-d_rti" class="skt-link">dṛti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rz_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृश्</span> <i>dṛś_1</i> var. <i>paś</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(paśyati)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>drakṣyati</i><i>)</i> pft. <i>(dadarśa)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <i>(adarśat)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(adīdṛśat)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] <i>(adrākṣīt)</i> inf. <i>(draṣṭum)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> ppft. <i>(dadṛśivas)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>dṛśya</i>, <i>darśanīya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>dṛṣṭvā</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>ni</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> voir; discerner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> regarder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aller voir, rendre visite à ⟨acc.⟩ — ps. <i>(dṛśyate)</i> être vu; apparaître, se manifester, se révéler; s'avérer comme, sembler être <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> impers. il est observé — ca. <i>(darśayati)</i> faire voir, montrer — int. md. <i>(darīdṛśyate)</i> être toujours visible; être constamment en évidence, être très souvent vu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être vu répétitivement — dés. <i>(didṛkṣati)</i> souhaiter voir — dés. md. <i>(didṛkṣate)</i> espérer voir, désirer.<br> <i>punaḥ</i> <i>drakṣyāmaḥ</i> adr. à bientôt.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rz_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृश्</span> <i>dṛś_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] <a href="#entry-d_rz_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> regard, vue, vision; œil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aspect, apparence, point de vue, doctrine — ifc. qui observe, qui comprend, qui sait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> semblable à, tel que.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृश</span> <i>dṛśa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-d_rza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_rza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_rzii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛśī</i> qui a l'apparence de, semblable à.<br> <i>īdṛśa</i> var. <i>etādṛśa</i>, <i>tādṛśa</i> tel.<br> <i>kīdṛśa</i> quel, de quelle nature, de quelle utilité?<br> <i>mādṛśa</i> tel que moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rzi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृशि</span> <i>dṛśi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] <a href="#entry-d_rzi" class="skt-link">f.</a> vision; faculté de la vue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rzvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृश्वन्</span> <i>dṛśvan</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-d_rzvan" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_rzvan" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_rzvanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛśvanī</i> qui a vu ⟨iic.⟩; familier de.<br> <i>merudṛśvā</i> qui a vu le mont <a href="#entry-Umeru" class="skt-link">Meru</a>.<br> <i>paralokadṛśvā</i> qui a vu l'au-delà.<br> <i>pāradṛśvā</i> qui a vu l'autre rive.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-d_rzya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दृश्य</span> <i>dṛśya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-d_rzya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-d_rzya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-d_rzyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dṛśyā</i> visible; à voir, plaisant — <a href="#entry-d_rzya" class="skt-link">n.</a> objet visible <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. drame (opp. <a href="#entry-zravya" class="skt-link">śravya</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द</span> <i>da</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dā</i> qui donne ⟨iic.⟩.<br> <i>sukhada</i> qui donne du bonheur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_m_s_traa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दंष्ट्रा</span> <i>daṃṣṭrā</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-da_mz" class="skt-link">daṃś</a>] <a href="#entry-da_m_s_traa" class="skt-link">f.</a> grosse dent, croc, défense <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uvahni_1" class="skt-link">Vahni_1</a>] np. de Daṃṣṭrā, épouse de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a>; elle engendra la race des tigres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_m_s_trin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṃṣṭrin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-da_m_s_trin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_m_s_trin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-da_m_s_tri_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daṃṣṭriṇī</i> (éléphant) muni de défenses.<br> <a href="#entry-da_m_s_traa" class="skt-link">daṃṣṭrā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_msana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दंसन</span> <i>daṃsana</i> <a href="#entry-da_msana" class="skt-link">n.</a> pouvoir merveilleux, exploit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>daṃsas</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_mz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दंश्</span> <i>daṃś</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(daśati)</i> pft. <i>(dadaṃśa)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>daṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> mordre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> piquer — ps. <i>(daśyate)</i> être mordu, être piqué — int. md. <i>(dandaśyate)</i> mordre mauvaisement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. δακνω.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_mza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दंश</span> <i>daṃśa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-da_mz" class="skt-link">daṃś</a>] <a href="#entry-da_mza" class="skt-link">m.</a> morsure, piqûre; endroit mordu, piqué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> insecte qui pique, taon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_mzana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दंशन</span> <i>daṃśana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-da_mz" class="skt-link">daṃś</a>] <a href="#entry-da_mzana" class="skt-link">n.</a> morsure, piqûre; fait de mordre, de piquer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> armure; harnais; équipement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_mzita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दंशित</span> <i>daṃśita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-da_mz" class="skt-link">daṃś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-da_mzita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_mzita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-da_mzitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daṃśitā</i> mordu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> harnassé, protégé par une armure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दण्ड</span> <i>daṇḍa</i> <a href="#entry-da_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> bâton, massue; manche; sceptre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mât; mesure de longueur (environ 2 m) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tige (not. de lotus) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> châtiment, punition; amende <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> force légitime, pouvoir de la justice; police, armée (en ligne) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. signe de ponctuation (|) marquant la fin d'une phrase ou la moitié d'une stance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Daṇḍa «Châtiment», fils de <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ukriyaa" class="skt-link">Kriyā</a> — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(daṇḍayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>daṇḍita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>daṇḍanīya</i><i>)</i> punir qqn. ⟨acc.⟩ avec qqc. ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. δενδρον.<br> <i>daṇḍaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> payer une amende <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> punir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_daasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍāsana</i> [<a href="#entry-aasana" class="skt-link">āsana</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_daasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] posture assise, jambes allongées sur le sol, mains de chaque côté du corps, doigts vers l'avant.<br> <a href="#entry-da_n_da" class="skt-link">daṇḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dabhaaj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍabhāj</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaaj" class="skt-link">bhāj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-da_n_dabhaaj" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_dabhaaj" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_dabhaaj" class="skt-link">f.</a> recevant la punition (par ⟨g.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dabhaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍabhaya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaya" class="skt-link">bhaya</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_dabhaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> peur du bâton; peur d'être puni.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dadhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍadhāra</i> [<a href="#entry-dhaara_1" class="skt-link">dhāra_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-da_n_dadhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_dadhaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_dadhaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daṇḍadhārā</i> qui administre la justice — <a href="#entry-da_n_dadhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dadhaara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍadhāraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-dhaara_na" class="skt-link">dhāraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_dadhaara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> port du sceptre ou du bâton <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [«administration du bâton»] punition.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dadhaaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍadhāraka</i> [<a href="#entry-dhaaraka" class="skt-link">dhāraka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-da_n_dadhaaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_dadhaaraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_dadhaarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daṇḍadhārikā</i> qui administre la justice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_daka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_daka" class="skt-link">m.</a> bâton <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ligne d'écriture <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Daṇḍaka «muni du Sceptre», fils d'<a href="#entry-Uik_svaaku" class="skt-link">Ikṣvāku</a>; son royaume fut transformé en une forêt — <a href="#entry-da_n_dakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daṇḍakā</i> géo. np. de la forêt [<a href="#entry-ara_nya" class="skt-link">araṇya</a>] Daṇḍakā, dans le Deccan, entre la <a href="#entry-Unarmadaa" class="skt-link">Narmadā</a> et la <a href="#entry-Ugodaavarii" class="skt-link">Godāvarī</a> (mod. Madhya Pradesh); myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> y vécut en ermite pendant son exil avec <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a> et <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dakaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दण्डका</span> <i>daṇḍakā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-da_n_daka" class="skt-link">daṇḍaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dakaa_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍakāṣṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-kaa_s_tha" class="skt-link">kāṣṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_dakaa_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. bâton de <a href="#entry-brahmacaarin" class="skt-link">brahmacārin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dakaara_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍakāraṇya</i> [<a href="#entry-ara_nya" class="skt-link">araṇya</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_dakaara_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. la forêt <a href="#entry-Uda_n_dakaa" class="skt-link">Daṇḍakā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-da_n_daka" class="skt-link">daṇḍaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dakaara_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍakāraṇya</i> [<a href="#entry-ara_nya" class="skt-link">araṇya</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_dakaara_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> forêt <a href="#entry-Uda_n_dakaa" class="skt-link">Daṇḍakā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dakaavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍakāvana</i> [<a href="#entry-vana" class="skt-link">vana</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_dakaavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> forêt <a href="#entry-Uda_n_dakaa" class="skt-link">Daṇḍakā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-da_n_dakaa" class="skt-link">daṇḍakā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_daniiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍanīti</i> [<a href="#entry-niiti" class="skt-link">nīti</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_daniiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> administration du châtiment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> maintien de l'ordre; justice administrée par le roi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> politique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_daniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दण्डनीय</span> <i>daṇḍanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-da_n_da" class="skt-link">daṇḍa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-da_n_daniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_daniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_daniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daṇḍanīyā</i> qui doit être puni.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dapaa_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍapāṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-paa_ni" class="skt-link">pāṇi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-da_n_dapaa_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_dapaa_ni" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_dapaa_ni" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a le sceptre en main; qui tient un bâton — <a href="#entry-da_n_dapaa_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> policier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dapaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍapāla</i> [<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_dapaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. surintendant des peines; bourreau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dapaaru_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍapāruṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-paaru_sya" class="skt-link">pāruṣya</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_dapaaru_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> châtiment brutal, justice sévère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dapaaru_syavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍapāruṣyavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-da_n_dapaaru_syavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_dapaaru_syavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_dapaaru_syavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daṇḍapāruṣyavatī</i> qui inflige un châtiment sévère.<br> <a href="#entry-da_n_dapaaru_sya" class="skt-link">daṇḍapāruṣya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_dapra_naama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍapraṇāma</i> [<a href="#entry-pra_naama" class="skt-link">praṇāma</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_dapra_naama" class="skt-link">m.</a> prosternation face contre terre en signe de soumission.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_davadha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍavadha</i> [<a href="#entry-vadha" class="skt-link">vadha</a>] <a href="#entry-da_n_davadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> peine capitale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_davat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daṇḍavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-da_n_davat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_davat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_davatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daṇḍavatī</i> qui porte un bâton <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> muni d'un manche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> raide comme un bâton <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ayant une grande armée — adv. comme un bâton <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en ligne droite.<br> <i>daṇḍavat</i> <i>praṇam</i> se prosterner face contre terre (allongé à plat ventre sur le sol, bras tendus croisés au niveau des poignets).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_n_din">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दण्डिन्</span> <i>daṇḍin</i> [<a href="#entry-da_n_da" class="skt-link">daṇḍa</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-da_n_din" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_din" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-da_n_dinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daṇḍinī</i> qui tient un bâton; armé d'un bâton <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> brâhmane au 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ stade de vie ascétique [<a href="#entry-sannyaasin" class="skt-link">sannyāsin</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ordre mendiant fondé par <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-dazanaamiida_n_din" class="skt-link">daśanāmīdaṇḍin</a> — <a href="#entry-da_n_din" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Daṇḍī «Justicier», serviteur de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a>; il punit les pécheurs et défend la justice divine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Daṇḍī_1, poète du 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, originaire de <a href="#entry-Ukaa_ncii" class="skt-link">Kāñcī</a>, auteur not. du traité de composition littéraire <a href="#entry-Ukaavyaadarza" class="skt-link">Kāvyādarśa</a> et du roman en prose <a href="#entry-Udazakumaaracarita" class="skt-link">Daśakumāracarita</a>; il est renommé pour sa maîtrise du <a href="#entry-sandhi" class="skt-link">sandhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-da_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दष्ट</span> <i>daṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-da_mz" class="skt-link">daṃś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-da_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-da_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-da_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daṣṭā</i> mordu, piqué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daa_dima">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाडिम</span> <i>dāḍima</i> <a href="#entry-daa_dima" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daa_dimii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāḍimī</i> grenadier — <a href="#entry-daa_dima" class="skt-link">n.</a> grenade.<br> <i>dāḍimāni</i> <i>daṃś</i> [«mordre dans une grenade»] faire une corvée, avoir une tâche infaisable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दा</span> <i>dā_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(dadāti)</i> pr. md. <i>(datte)</i> imp. <i>(dehi)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(dadati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(dāti)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>dāsyati</i><i>)</i> pft. <i>(dadau)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <i>(adāt)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(adīdadat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>datta</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(dadat)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>dātavya</i>, <i>dānīya</i>, <i>deya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>dattvā</i>, <i>-dāya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> donner, accorder, fournir, offrir qqc. ⟨acc.⟩ à qqn. ⟨dat. g. loc.⟩ — ps. <i>(dīyate)</i> être donné — ca. <i>(dāpayati)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(adīdapat)</i> faire donner, faire payer, recouvrer — dés. <i>(ditsati)</i> vouloir donner, être prêt à donner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>do</i>; fr. donner.<br> <i>agnīn</i> <i>dā</i> disposer les feux du sacrifice.<br> <i>anuyātrāṃ</i> <i>dā</i> accompagner.<br> <i>argalaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> tirer le verrou.<br> <i>avakāśaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> faire de la place, laisser passer.<br> <i>ājñāṃ</i> <i>dā</i> donner un ordre.<br> <i>ātmānaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> se sacrifier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se donner à, se consacrer à ⟨dat.⟩.<br> <i>āsanaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> céder sa place.<br> <i>ṛṇaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> payer sa dette.<br> <i>karṇaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> prêter l'oreille.<br> <i>jānu</i> <i>dā</i> s'agenouiller <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mettre le genou sur le corps.<br> <i>talaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> gifler.<br> <i>daṇḍaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> payer l'amende <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> punir.<br> <i>dṛṣṭiṃ</i> <i>dā</i> jeter un coup d'œil.<br> <i>mano</i> <i>dā</i> fixer l'esprit sur ⟨loc.⟩.<br> <i>mārgaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> céder le chemin.<br> <i>vacaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> dire une parole, parler.<br> <i>varaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> accorder un don.<br> <i>viśaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> empoisonner.<br> <i>śabdaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> élever la voix, appeler.<br> <i>śokaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> causer du chagrin.<br> <i>saṃdeśaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> donner un renseignement, informer.<br> <i>samayaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> proposer un accord, convenir.<br> <i>prema</i> <i>bhikṣāṃ</i> <i>dehi</i> Donne nous l'Amour Divin en aumône.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दा</span> <i>dā_2</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(dāti)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(dyati)</i> pft. <i>(dadau)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <i>(adāt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ava</i>, <i>ni</i><i>)</i> diviser, partager <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> couper; mettre en pièces, déchirer, détruire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lier — ps. <i>(dīyate)</i> être rejeté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daa_3">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दा</span> <i>dā_3</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(dāyati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ava</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाद</span> <i>dāda</i> [<a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-daada" class="skt-link">m.</a> cadeau; don.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daadada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dādada</i> [<a href="#entry-da" class="skt-link">da</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daadada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daadada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daadadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dādadā</i> qui donne des cadeaux.<br> <a href="#entry-daada" class="skt-link">dāda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाह</span> <i>dāha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dah_1" class="skt-link">dah_1</a>] <a href="#entry-daaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> incendie; brûlure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाक्ष</span> <i>dākṣa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dak_sa" class="skt-link">dakṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_sii_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dākṣī_1</i> myth. relatif à <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méridional — <a href="#entry-daak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> le Sud.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daak_saaya_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dākṣāyaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-aayana" class="skt-link">āyana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daak_saaya_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_saaya_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_saaya_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dākṣāyaṇī</i> myth. descendant de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>; relatif à <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a> — <a href="#entry-daak_saaya_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dākṣāyaṇī</i> myth. np. de Dākṣāyaṇī «Fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>», épith. not. de <a href="#entry-Usatii" class="skt-link">Satī</a> et des <a href="#entry-nak_satraa_ni" class="skt-link">nakṣatrāṇi</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uau_sadhii" class="skt-link">Auṣadhī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-daak_sa" class="skt-link">dākṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daak_si">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाक्षि</span> <i>dākṣi</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dak_sa" class="skt-link">dakṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-daak_si" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_sii_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dākṣī_2</i> [<a href="#entry-gotra" class="skt-link">gotra</a>] descendant de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daak_si_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाक्षिण</span> <i>dākṣiṇa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dak_si_naa" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daak_si_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_si_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dākṣiṇī</i> soc. relatif aux honoraires du prêtre — <a href="#entry-daak_si_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dākṣiṇā</i> géo. l'Inde du Sud, le Deccan.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daak_si_naatya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाक्षिणात्य</span> <i>dākṣiṇātya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dak_si_naa" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇā</a><a href="#entry--tya" class="skt-link">-tya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daak_si_naatya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_si_naatya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_si_naatyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dākṣiṇātyā</i> du Sud, méridional; not. issu du Deccan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. habitants du Deccan.<br> <i>priyataddhitā</i> <i>dākṣiṇātyāḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-Upata_njali_1" class="skt-link">Patañjali_1</a>] Les indiens du Sud sont friands de suffixes secondaires.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daak_si_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाक्षिण्य</span> <i>dākṣiṇya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dak_si_naa" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇā</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daak_si_nya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_si_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daak_si_nyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dākṣiṇyā</i> qui concerne ou qui mérite le paiement d'honoraires rituels — <a href="#entry-daak_si_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> dextérité, habileté, adresse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> politesse, considération, piété.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daak_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाक्ष्य</span> <i>dākṣya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dak_sa" class="skt-link">dakṣa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-daak_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> habileté, adresse; capacité; zèle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाम</span> <i>dāma</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-daaman_2" class="skt-link">dāman_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaman_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दामन्</span> <i>dāman_1</i> [<a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-daaman_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> donneur; homme généreux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaman_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दामन्</span> <i>dāman_2</i> [<a href="#entry-daa_2" class="skt-link">dā_2</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-daaman_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> lien, corde, entrave; ceinture <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> guirlande; collier, chapelet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daamodara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāmodara</i> [<a href="#entry-udara" class="skt-link">udara</a>] <a href="#entry-daamodara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Dāmodara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> enfant «attaché par une corde autour de sa taille» (par sa nourrice <a href="#entry-Uyazodaa" class="skt-link">Yaśodā</a> pour ses espiègleries).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daamodaraa_s_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāmodarāṣṭaka</i> [<a href="#entry-a_s_taka" class="skt-link">aṣṭaka</a>] <a href="#entry-daamodaraa_s_taka" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. festival célébrant <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>-<a href="#entry-Udaamodara" class="skt-link">Dāmodara</a>, le 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour du mois <a href="#entry-kaarttika" class="skt-link">kārttika</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-gopaa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">gopāṣṭamī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-daamodara" class="skt-link">dāmodara</a> <br> <a href="#entry-daama" class="skt-link">dāma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daamodaragupta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāmodaragupta</i> [<a href="#entry-gupta" class="skt-link">gupta</a>] <a href="#entry-daamodaragupta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Dāmodaragupta, poète et premier ministre du roi du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> <a href="#entry-Ujayaapii_da" class="skt-link">Jayāpīḍa</a> (fin 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Uku_t_taniimata" class="skt-link">Kuṭṭanīmata</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दान</span> <i>dāna</i> [act. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-daana" class="skt-link">n.</a> don, présent; générosité, libéralité; charité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. la générosité, une des 6 perfections [<a href="#entry-paaramitaa" class="skt-link">pāramitā</a>] d'un accompli [<a href="#entry-bodhisattva" class="skt-link">bodhisattva</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> liquide exhudant des tempes de l'éléphant en rut.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daanadharma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dānadharma</i> [<a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a>] <a href="#entry-daanadharma" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. devoir de charité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daanastuti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dānastuti</i> [<a href="#entry-stuti" class="skt-link">stuti</a>] <a href="#entry-daanastuti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [RV.] louange pour le don, remerciement rituel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daanava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दानव</span> <i>dānava</i> [<a href="#entry-daanu" class="skt-link">dānu</a>] <a href="#entry-daanava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daanavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dānavī</i> myth. [«fils de <a href="#entry-Udanu" class="skt-link">Danu</a>»] démon ondin, variété d'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> aquatique sous les ordres de <a href="#entry-Uraahu" class="skt-link">Rāhu</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uv_r_saparvan" class="skt-link">Vṛṣaparvan</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>dānavās</i> la classe de ces démons; ils sont fils de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et <a href="#entry-Udanu" class="skt-link">Danu</a>; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> demanda à <a href="#entry-Udu_smanta" class="skt-link">Duṣmanta</a> de les combattre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. cf. <a href="#entry-Udanu_1" class="skt-link">Danu_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daanavapati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dānavapati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-daanavapati" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Dānavapati, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uraahu" class="skt-link">Rāhu</a> «Roi des <a href="#entry-daanavaas" class="skt-link">dānavās</a>».<br> <a href="#entry-daanava" class="skt-link">dānava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दानीय</span> <i>dānīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dānīyā</i> qui doit être donné ou offert.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daanin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dānin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daanin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daanin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daaninii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāninī</i> qui donne; généreux.<br> <a href="#entry-daana" class="skt-link">dāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दान्त</span> <i>dānta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-dam_1" class="skt-link">dam_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāntā</i> dompté, dressé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a dompté ses passions.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daanu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दानु</span> <i>dānu</i> [<a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a><a href="#entry--nu" class="skt-link">-nu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daanu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daanu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daanu" class="skt-link">f.</a> libéral <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> courageux; vaillant, victorieux, conquérant — <a href="#entry-daanu" class="skt-link">m.</a> donateur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prospérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vent, courant d'air <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> eau, courant de fluide.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाप्</span> <i>dāp</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(dāpayati)</i> faire donner, faire payer, recouvrer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daar_dhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दार्ढ्य</span> <i>dārḍhya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-d_r_dha" class="skt-link">dṛḍha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daar_dhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daar_dhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daar_dhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dārḍhyā</i> solidité, résistance; stabilité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दार</span> <i>dāra</i> <a href="#entry-daara" class="skt-link">m.</a> épouse (rare) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>dārās</i> (collectif) les épouses.<br> <i>darān</i> <i>kṛ</i> var. <i>darānprakṛ</i> prendre pour épouse, épouser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-daaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> fils, jeune garçon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>dārakau</i> garçon et fille — <a href="#entry-daarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dārikā</i> fillette.<br> <a href="#entry-daara" class="skt-link">dāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaridrya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दारिद्र्य</span> <i>dāridrya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-daridr" class="skt-link">daridr</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-daaridrya" class="skt-link">n.</a> pauvreté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daarteya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दार्तेय</span> <i>dārteya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-d_rti" class="skt-link">dṛti</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daarteya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daarteya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daarteyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dārteyā</i> qui se trouve dans une outre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दारु</span> <i>dāru</i> [<a href="#entry-dru_3" class="skt-link">dru_3</a>] <a href="#entry-daaru" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daaru" class="skt-link">n.</a> bois; morceau de bois; poteau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaru_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दारुण</span> <i>dāruṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-daaru" class="skt-link">dāru</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daaru_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daaru_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daaru_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāruṇā</i> <a href="#entry-daaru_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāruṇī</i> dur, rude; sévère, implacable; intense; cruel, violent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pénible, douloureux; effrayant, terrible, épouvantable — <a href="#entry-daaru_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> dureté, rudesse, sévérité; horreur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daarubrahman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dārubrahman</i> [<a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>] <a href="#entry-daarubrahman" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Dārubrahmā, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a> «le Dieu de bois».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaruka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दारुक</span> <i>dāruka</i> [<a href="#entry-daaru" class="skt-link">dāru</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-daaruka" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Cedrus deodara</i>, cèdre de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daarukavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dārukavana</i> [<a href="#entry-vana" class="skt-link">vana</a>] <a href="#entry-daarukavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. de la forêt de cèdres Dārukavana, où vivaient de nombreux ermites [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>]; ils étaient infatués de leurs rituels Védiques et ne vénéraient que le <a href="#entry-Uzabdabrahman" class="skt-link">Śabdabrahman</a>; <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> visita l'ermitage comme mendiant nu sous sa forme de <a href="#entry-Ubhik_saa_tana" class="skt-link">Bhikṣāṭana</a>, pour humilier ces hérétiques; les épouses des sages tombèrent amoureuses de sa grande beauté; les sages voulurent le faire disparaître, et firent surgir un tigre, qu'il tua et dont il se para de sa peau; ils firent surgir un daim, qu'il prit dans sa main gauche comme attribut; ils produisirent un fer chauffé au rouge, qu'il garda en main comme arme; un démon-éléphant [<a href="#entry-gajaasura" class="skt-link">gajāsura</a>] nommé <a href="#entry-Ugaya_1" class="skt-link">Gaya_1</a> ayant acquis de grands pouvoirs par ses austérités fit fuir les sages à <a href="#entry-Uvaaraa_nasii" class="skt-link">Vārāṇasī</a>, où ils se réfugièrent dans un temple de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; celui-ci tua le démon-éléphant avec son trident, et se para de sa dépouille comme d'une cape.<br> <a href="#entry-daaruka" class="skt-link">dāruka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daarumaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dārumaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daarumaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daarumaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daarumayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dārumayī</i> en bois.<br> <i>dārumayī</i> <i>pratimā</i> idole en bois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaruzilaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāruśilā</i> [<a href="#entry-zilaa" class="skt-link">śilā</a>] <a href="#entry-daaruzilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> bois et pierre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaruzilaagraha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāruśilāgrahaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-graha_na" class="skt-link">grahaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-daaruzilaagraha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. critères de sélection des matériaux destinés aux images sacrées.<br> <a href="#entry-daaruzilaa" class="skt-link">dāruśilā</a> <br> <a href="#entry-daaru" class="skt-link">dāru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daarva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दार्व</span> <i>dārva</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-daaru" class="skt-link">dāru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daarva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daarva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daarvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dārvī</i> en bois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दास</span> <i>dāsa</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-dasyu" class="skt-link">dasyu</a>] <a href="#entry-daasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> esclave, serviteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pêcheur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. [«serviteur de Dieu»] np. de Dāsa, suffixe du nom de renonçants du courant dévotionnel [<a href="#entry-bhakti" class="skt-link">bhakti</a>] de la tradition <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a> — <a href="#entry-daasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāsī</i> femme esclave, servante <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> femme de pêcheur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daasakarmakara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāsakarmakara</i> [<a href="#entry-karmakara" class="skt-link">karmakara</a>] <a href="#entry-daasakarmakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. soc. les serfs et les travailleurs libres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daasaraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāsarāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-daasaraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Dāsarāja, «Chef des pêcheurs» de la <a href="#entry-Uyamunaa" class="skt-link">Yamunā</a>, qui éleva <a href="#entry-Usatyavatii" class="skt-link">Satyavatī</a> et la donna en mariage au roi <a href="#entry-Uzaantanu" class="skt-link">Śāṃtanu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दासित</span> <i>dāsita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-das" class="skt-link">das</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāsitā</i> épuisé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāsya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-daasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> esclavage, servitude.<br> <a href="#entry-daasa" class="skt-link">dāsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daasyaa_hputra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāsyāḥputra</i> var. <i>dāsīputra</i> [<a href="#entry-putra" class="skt-link">putra</a>] <a href="#entry-daasyaa_hputra" class="skt-link">m.</a> fils de servante, bâtard (injure).<br> <a href="#entry-daasyaas" class="skt-link">dāsyās</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daasyaas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दास्यास्</span> <i>dāsyās</i> [g. <a href="#entry-daasii" class="skt-link">dāsī</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daat_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दातृ</span> <i>dātṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daat_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daat_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daatrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dātrī</i> qui accorde, qui permet; qui donne, qui prête; libéral, généreux; donateur, mécène, fondateur; prêteur, créancier — <a href="#entry-daatrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dātrī</i> généreuse, donatrice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daatavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दातव्य</span> <i>dātavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daatavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daatavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daatavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dātavyā</i> qui doit être donné ou offert.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दात्र</span> <i>dātra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-daa_2" class="skt-link">dā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-daatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> faucille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाव</span> <i>dāva</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-du" class="skt-link">du</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daavaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāvā</i> qui brûle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> forêt, parc — <a href="#entry-daava" class="skt-link">m.</a> incendie, not. d'une forêt.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaya_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाय</span> <i>dāya_1</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daaya_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daaya_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāyā</i> ifc. qui donne ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-daaya_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> don, cadeau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. héritage, dot; don au maître après les études.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaya_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाय</span> <i>dāya_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-daa_2" class="skt-link">dā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-daaya_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> portion, part; part d'héritage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> division; destruction — ifc. division en ⟨iic.⟩ parts.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daayaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāyāda</i> [<a href="#entry-aada" class="skt-link">āda</a>] <a href="#entry-daayaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> héritier (de ⟨g. loc iic.⟩) — <a href="#entry-daayaadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāyādā</i> héritière — <a href="#entry-daayaadii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāyādī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daayaadya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāyādya</i> [<a href="#entry-ya" class="skt-link">ya</a>] <a href="#entry-daayaadya" class="skt-link">n.</a> héritage.<br> <a href="#entry-daayaada" class="skt-link">dāyāda</a> <br> <a href="#entry-daaya_1" class="skt-link">dāya_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daayabhaaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāyabhāga</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaaga" class="skt-link">bhāga</a>] <a href="#entry-daayabhaaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. partage d'un héritage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. traité hindou sur l'héritage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daayabhaagaprabodhinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāyabhāgaprabodhinī</i> [<a href="#entry-prabodhinii" class="skt-link">prabodhinī</a>] <a href="#entry-daayabhaagaprabodhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Dāyabhāgaprabodhinī «Manuel de droit d'héritage» de Śrīkṛṣṇa Trakālaṅkāra.<br> <a href="#entry-daayabhaaga" class="skt-link">dāyabhāga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daayaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दायक</span> <i>dāyaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daayaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daayaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daayikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāyikā</i> qui donne; qui octroie — ifc. qui cause, qui permet, qui affecte ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaz_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाश्</span> <i>dāś_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(dāśati)</i> pft. <i>(dadāśa)</i> véd. honorer un dieu ⟨acc. dat.⟩ avec ⟨i.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. donner, accorder à ⟨dat.⟩ — ca. <i>(dāśayati)</i> offrir, donner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaz_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाश्</span> <i>dāś_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-daaz_1" class="skt-link">dāś_1</a>] <a href="#entry-daaz_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. véd. culte, vénération — <a href="#entry-daaz_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. dévot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाश</span> <i>dāśa</i> [<a href="#entry-daaz_1" class="skt-link">dāś_1</a>] <a href="#entry-daaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> donataire (à qui l'on donne) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pêcheur; batelier; cf. <a href="#entry-daasa" class="skt-link">dāsa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daazaraaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाशराज्ञ</span> <i>dāśarājña</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-daza" class="skt-link">daśa</a>-<a href="#entry-raajan" class="skt-link">rājan</a>] <a href="#entry-daazaraaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. «(bataille) des 10 rois», remportée par <a href="#entry-Usudaasa" class="skt-link">Sudāsa</a>, chef de la tribu des <a href="#entry-Ubharataas" class="skt-link">Bharatās</a>, grâce à l'aide du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a>; ces rois menaient respectivement 5 tribus aryennes et 5 tribus non aryennes; la bataille eut lieu près de la rivière <a href="#entry-Uparu_s_nii" class="skt-link">Paruṣṇī</a>; elle donna aux <a href="#entry-Ubharataas" class="skt-link">Bharatās</a> la souveraineté sur le <a href="#entry-Ukuruk_setra" class="skt-link">Kurukṣetra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daazaratha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाशरथ</span> <i>dāśaratha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dazaratha" class="skt-link">daśaratha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daazaratha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daazaratha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daazarathaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāśarathā</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Udazaratha" class="skt-link">Daśaratha</a> — <a href="#entry-daazaratha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Dāśaratha, patr. de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> — <a href="#entry-daazarathi" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>dāśarathi</i> myth. id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>dāśarathī</i> myth. <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> et <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daazaspati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāśaspati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-daazaspati" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [«maître des offrandes»] grand donateur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daazaspatya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dāśaspatya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daazaspatya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daazaspatya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daazaspatyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dāśaspatyā</i> prolifique; not. bonne laitière (vache).<br> <a href="#entry-daazaspati" class="skt-link">dāśaspati</a> <br> <a href="#entry-daaz_2" class="skt-link">dāś_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daazvas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दाश्वस्</span> <i>dāśvas</i> [ppft. <a href="#entry-daaz_1" class="skt-link">dāś_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daazvas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daazvas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daazvas" class="skt-link">f.</a> dévot, pieux — <a href="#entry-daazvas" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. qualifie différents dieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dabh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दभ्</span> <i>dabh</i> var. <i>dambh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(dabhati)</i> tromper, décevoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दद्</span> <i>dad</i> cf. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दद</span> <i>dada</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-dada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dadā</i> qui donne.<br> <i>dhanaṃdadā</i> f. bd. déesse accordant la richesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dadat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ददत्</span> <i>dadat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dadat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dadat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dadatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dadatī</i> donnant, qui donne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dadhaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दधान</span> <i>dadhāna</i> [ppr. md. <a href="#entry-dhaa_1" class="skt-link">dhā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dadhaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dadhaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dadhaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dadhānā</i> tenant, portant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dadhan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दधन्</span> <i>dadhan</i> [<a href="#entry-dhaa_1" class="skt-link">dhā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-dadhan" class="skt-link">n.</a> lait caillé, petit-beurre; yaourt <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>dadhi</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>dohi</i>.<br> <i>dadhnā</i> <i>juhoti</i> on donne du caillé en oblation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dadhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दधि</span> <i>dadhi</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-dadhan" class="skt-link">dadhan</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dadhiica">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dadhīca</i> var. <i>dadhīci</i>, <i>dadhyañc</i> [<a href="#entry-iic" class="skt-link">īc</a>] <a href="#entry-dadhiica" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. véd. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Dadhīca ou Dadhīci ou <a href="#entry-Udadhyaf" class="skt-link">Dadhyaṅ</a> «Qui aime ou répand le <a href="#entry-dadhi" class="skt-link">dadhi</a>», fils de <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] ou d'<a href="#entry-Uatharvaa" class="skt-link">Atharvā</a> [BP.]; il vivait au bord de la <a href="#entry-Usabarmatii" class="skt-link">Sabarmatī</a>; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> lui révéla des mystères qu'il ne devait pas dévoiler sous peine d'avoir la tête tranchée; les Cavaliers jumeaux [<a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a>] lui donnèrent une tête de cheval, et il leur apprit à trouver le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> dans la maison de <a href="#entry-Utva_s_taa" class="skt-link">Tvaṣṭā</a> et à reconstituer le sacrifice <a href="#entry-Umakha" class="skt-link">Makha</a> décapité; quand <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> lui coupa la tête, les Cavaliers lui rendirent sa tête d'origine; il donna les os de sa tête de cheval à <a href="#entry-Utva_s_taa" class="skt-link">Tvaṣṭā</a> pour forger le <a href="#entry-vajra" class="skt-link">vajra</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> qui servit à tuer <a href="#entry-Uv_rtra" class="skt-link">Vṛtra</a>; il se sacrifie pour les justes causes; on lui attribue l'invention du miel; lorsque <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> essaya de l'attaquer avec <a href="#entry-Usudarzana" class="skt-link">Sudarśana</a>, celui-ci perdit son tranchant et devint aussi inoffensif qu'une roue de potier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dadhikraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dadhikrā</i> [<a href="#entry-kraa" class="skt-link">krā</a>] <a href="#entry-dadhikraa" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. [RV.] np. du cheval céleste Dadhikrā ou Dadhikrāvan; il personnifie le Soleil levant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>dadhikrāvan</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dadhipayas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dadhipayas</i> [<a href="#entry-payas" class="skt-link">payas</a>] <a href="#entry-dadhipayas" class="skt-link">n.</a> du. <i>dadhipayasī</i> lait et petit-lait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dadhyac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dadhyac</i> var. <i>dadhīca</i> [<a href="#entry-ac" class="skt-link">ac</a>] <a href="#entry-dadhyac" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. véd. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Dadhyaṅ «Qui répand le <a href="#entry-dadhi" class="skt-link">dadhi</a>»; cf. <a href="#entry-Udadhiici" class="skt-link">Dadhīci</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dadhyodana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dadhyodana</i> [<a href="#entry-odana" class="skt-link">odana</a>] <a href="#entry-dadhyodana" class="skt-link">n.</a> mélange de riz cuit et de lait caillé.<br> <a href="#entry-dadhi" class="skt-link">dadhi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dadru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दद्रु</span> <i>dadru</i> <a href="#entry-dadru" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. tortue — <a href="#entry-dadruu" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dadrū</i> dermatose; lèpre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dadruka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dadruka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-dadruka" class="skt-link">m.</a> dermatose; lèpre.<br> <a href="#entry-dadru" class="skt-link">dadru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dafk_s_nu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दङ्क्ष्णु</span> <i>daṅkṣṇu</i> [<a href="#entry-da_mz" class="skt-link">daṃś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dafk_s_nu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dafk_s_nu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dafk_s_nu" class="skt-link">f.</a> mordant, piquant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dagdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दग्ध</span> <i>dagdha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-dah_1" class="skt-link">dah_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dagdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dagdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dagdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dagdhā</i> consumé; brûlé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tourmenté; en détresse; misérable, malchanceux — <a href="#entry-dagdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> cautérisation — <a href="#entry-dagdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dagdhā</i> direction du Soleil.<br> <i>dagdhajihva</i> a. m. n. qui a brûlé sa langue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dagdhadaiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dagdhadaiva</i> [<a href="#entry-daiva" class="skt-link">daiva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dagdhadaiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dagdhadaiva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dagdhadaivaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dagdhadaivā</i> accablé par le destin.<br> <a href="#entry-dagdha" class="skt-link">dagdha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dagh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दघ्</span> <i>dagh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(daghnoti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>daghna</i><i>)</i> atteindre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daghna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दघ्न</span> <i>daghna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-dagh" class="skt-link">dagh</a>] ifc. après <i>(</i><i>aśva</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>upakakṣa</i>, <i>upastha</i>, <i>ūru</i>, <i>kaṇṭha</i>, <i>kulpha</i>, <i>gaja</i>, <i>gulpha</i>, <i>jānu</i>, <i>nābhi</i>, <i>puruṣa</i>, <i>mukha</i>, <i>stana</i><i>)</i> a. <a href="#entry-daghna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daghna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daghnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daghnā</i> <a href="#entry-daghnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daghnī</i> jusqu'à hauteur de ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dah_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दह्</span> <i>dah_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(dahati)</i> pr. md. <i>(dahate)</i> pft. <i>(dadāha)</i> pft. md. <i>(dehe)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>dhakṣyati</i><i>)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(adīdahat)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] <i>(adhākṣīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dagdha</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(dahat)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>dagdhvā</i>, <i>-dahya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>nis</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> brûler, consumer, mettre au feu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (au fig.) détruire — ps. <i>(dahyate)</i> être consumé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être brûlant — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(dahyati)</i> brûler, être en feu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (au fig.) être consumé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'affliger, se tourmenter — ca. <i>(dāhayati)</i> faire brûler — int. <i>(dandahīti)</i> détruire par le feu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. day; all. Tag.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dah_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दह्</span> <i>dah_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dah_1" class="skt-link">dah_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-dah_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dah_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dah_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui brûle; brûlant.<br> <i>grāmadhak</i> qui brûle le village.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dahana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दहन</span> <i>dahana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dah_1" class="skt-link">dah_1</a>] <a href="#entry-dahana" class="skt-link">m.</a> feu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Dahana «Brûlant», l'un des 11 souffles divins [<a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>]; épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> — <a href="#entry-dahana" class="skt-link">n.</a> action de brûler, combustion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. «brûlure d'amour», l'une des 5 flèches érotiques [<a href="#entry-pu_spabaa_na" class="skt-link">puṣpabāṇa</a>] de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> — ifc. qui brûle, qui consume, qui calcine, qui détruit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mise au feu de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dahat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दहत्</span> <i>dahat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-dah_1" class="skt-link">dah_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dahat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dahat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dahantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dahantī</i> ardent.<br> <i>dahannaiva</i> <i>diśo</i> <i>daśa</i> myth. [BhP.] Brûlante dans toutes les directions (dit de la fièvre que <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> envoya à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, qui lui envoya en retour une fièvre glaçante [<a href="#entry-ziita" class="skt-link">śīta</a>]).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daidhi_savya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दैधिषव्य</span> <i>daidhiṣavya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-didhi_suu" class="skt-link">didhiṣū</a>] <a href="#entry-daidhi_savya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. fils d'un second mariage d'une veuve.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daihika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दैहिक</span> <i>daihika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-deha" class="skt-link">deha</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daihika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daihika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daihikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daihikā</i> concernant le corps; corporel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दैन</span> <i>daina</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dina" class="skt-link">dina</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dainii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dainī</i> de jour, diurne; quotidien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dainam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दैनम्</span> <i>dainam</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-daina" class="skt-link">daina</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dainandina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
