    <i>dainaṃdina</i> [<a href="#entry-dina" class="skt-link">dina</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daina_mdina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daina_mdina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daina_mdinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dainaṃdinī</i> quotidien.<br> <a href="#entry-dainam" class="skt-link">dainam</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dainika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दैनिक</span> <i>dainika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dina" class="skt-link">dina</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dainika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dainika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dainikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dainikī</i> par jour; de jour; quotidien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dainya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दैन्य</span> <i>dainya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-diina" class="skt-link">dīna</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-dainya" class="skt-link">m.</a> tristesse, découragement; détresse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faiblesse, lâcheté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daitya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दैत्य</span> <i>daitya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-diti" class="skt-link">diti</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-daitya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. fils de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et <a href="#entry-Uditi" class="skt-link">Diti</a>, <a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> perturbant les sacrifices; <a href="#entry-Ubali" class="skt-link">Bali</a> est le roi des <i>daityās</i> — a. <a href="#entry-daitya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daitya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daityaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daityā</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Uditi" class="skt-link">Diti</a> et à ses fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daityasenaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daityasenā</i> [<a href="#entry-senaa" class="skt-link">senā</a>] <a href="#entry-daityasenaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Daityasenā «Armée des démons», fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, sœur de <a href="#entry-Udevasenaa" class="skt-link">Devasenā</a>; <a href="#entry-Ukezii" class="skt-link">Keśī</a> l'enleva pour en faire son épouse.<br> <a href="#entry-daitya" class="skt-link">daitya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दैव</span> <i>daiva</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daiva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daivii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daivī</i> divin, céleste, provenant des dieux; fatal — <a href="#entry-daiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«ce qui procède du divin»] volonté divine; destin, sort; hasard, fatalité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>divinus</i>; fr. devin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daivahata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daivahata</i> [<a href="#entry-hata" class="skt-link">hata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daivahata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daivahata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daivahataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daivahatā</i> frappé par le sort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daivahataka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daivahataka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daivahataka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daivahataka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daivahatakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daivahatakā</i> frappé par le sort — <a href="#entry-daivahataka" class="skt-link">n.</a> coup du sort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> destin tragique.<br> <a href="#entry-daivahata" class="skt-link">daivahata</a> <br> <a href="#entry-daiva" class="skt-link">daiva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daivaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daivajña</i> [<a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">jña</a>] <a href="#entry-daivaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> devin [«qui connaît le destin»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Daivajña, titre d'astrologue prédictif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daivata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दैवत</span> <i>daivata</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-devataa" class="skt-link">devatā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daivata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daivata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daivataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daivatā</i> qui concerne les dieux — <a href="#entry-daivata" class="skt-link">n.</a> divinité; idole — ifc. qui adore.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daivika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दैविक</span> <i>daivika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daivika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daivika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daivakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daivakī</i> divin — <a href="#entry-daivika" class="skt-link">n.</a> fatalité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daizika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दैशिक</span> <i>daiśika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-deza" class="skt-link">deśa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daizika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daizika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daizikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daiśikā</i> spatial.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दक्ष्</span> <i>dakṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(dakṣate)</i> pft. <i>(dadakṣe)</i> véd. être utile, être capable, servir; renforcer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दक्ष</span> <i>dakṣa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dak_s" class="skt-link">dakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dak_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dakṣā</i> capable, adroit, habile, compétent; expert en ⟨loc.⟩; zélé — <a href="#entry-dak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> adéquation, efficacité; compétence, expertise <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du radieux [<a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">āditya</a>] Dakṣa «Expert (en rituel)», dieu du sacrifice, issu du pouce de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>, aussi appelé Pracetā «Savant»; il est à la fois père et fils d'<a href="#entry-Uaditi" class="skt-link">Aditi</a>, et père de <a href="#entry-Uditi" class="skt-link">Diti</a>; chef des géniteurs [<a href="#entry-prajaapati" class="skt-link">prajāpati</a>], il personnifie le rituel et la magie efficace; il est père par <a href="#entry-Uasiknii" class="skt-link">Asiknī</a> de <a href="#entry-Usatii" class="skt-link">Satī</a>, l'épouse de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, qu'il refusa d'inviter à un sacrifice dédié à <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, car il était impur comme <a href="#entry-Ukapaalii" class="skt-link">Kapālī</a>; <a href="#entry-Uviirabhadra" class="skt-link">Vīrabhadra</a>-<a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> détruisit ce sacrifice, dans le feu duquel se jeta <a href="#entry-Usatii" class="skt-link">Satī</a>; décapité par le <a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a> fut rendu à la vie avec une tête de bouc; il donna en mariage 13 de ses filles à <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a>, 10 à <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a>, 27 à <a href="#entry-Usoma" class="skt-link">Soma</a>-<a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a> (les astérismes [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>]), 4 à <a href="#entry-Uari_s_tanemi" class="skt-link">Ariṣṭanemi</a> et sa cadette <a href="#entry-Usatii" class="skt-link">Satī</a> à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; il eut également 24 filles de son épouse <a href="#entry-Uprasuuti" class="skt-link">Prasūti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_sasaavar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣasāvarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-saavar_na" class="skt-link">sāvarṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_sasaavar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Dakṣasāvarṇa, 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a> du <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-dak_sa" class="skt-link">dakṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_sayaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣayajña</i> [<a href="#entry-yaj_na" class="skt-link">yajña</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_sayaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. le sacrifice de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>; <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, qui en fut exclu, le détruisit de sa colère personnifiée en <a href="#entry-Uviirabhadra" class="skt-link">Vīrabhadra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_sayaj_navinaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣayajñavināśa</i> [<a href="#entry-vinaaza" class="skt-link">vināśa</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_sayaj_navinaaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. destruction du sacrifice de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-dak_sayaj_na" class="skt-link">dakṣayajña</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दक्षिण</span> <i>dakṣiṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-dak_s" class="skt-link">dakṣ</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dak_si_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dak_si_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dak_si_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dakṣiṇā</i> à droite; opp. <a href="#entry-savya" class="skt-link">savya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méridional, vers le Sud [«à main droite (en faisant face à l'Est)»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> droit, franc, sincère — <a href="#entry-dak_si_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dak_si_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> le Sud — f. cf. <a href="#entry-dak_si_naa" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>dexter</i>; pkt. <i>Dekkan</i>.<br> <i>dakṣiṇam</i> adv. à droite.<br> <i>dakṣiṇā</i> adv. à droite, au Sud.<br> <i>dakṣiṇena</i> adv. id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à droite de ⟨acc. g.⟩.<br> <i>dakṣiṇāt</i> adv. issu de la droite; venant du Sud.<br> <i>dakṣiṇe</i> adv. du côté droit.<br> <i>dakṣiṇā</i> <i>diś</i> la direction du Sud.<br> <i>dakṣiṇaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> placer qqn. à sa droite (en signe de respect).<br> <i>dakṣiṇīkṛ</i> mettre à droite, au Sud.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_naa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दक्षिणा</span> <i>dakṣiṇā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-dak_si_na" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_si_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> la direction [<a href="#entry-dik" class="skt-link">dik</a>] du Sud <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. [«prix du service»] honoraire payé au prêtre par le sacrifiant; don, donation, aumône <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> not. don d'une bonne vache laitière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_naacaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣiṇācāra</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">ācāra</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_si_naacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] la voie tantrique [<a href="#entry-zaakta" class="skt-link">śākta</a>] «droite» ou orthodoxe; opp. <a href="#entry-vaamaacaara" class="skt-link">vāmācāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_naagni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣiṇāgni</i> [<a href="#entry-agni" class="skt-link">agni</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_si_naagni" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. feu méridional du sacrifice védique; syn. <a href="#entry-anvaahaaryapacana" class="skt-link">anvāhāryapacana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [BhP.] np. de Dakṣiṇāgni, monstre de feu créé par <a href="#entry-Usudak_si_na" class="skt-link">Sudakṣiṇa</a>, grâce à un <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> que lui avait donné <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; il échoua à combattre <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, et finit par détruire son créateur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_naamnaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣiṇāmnāya</i> [<a href="#entry-aamnaaya" class="skt-link">āmnāya</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_si_naamnaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [«le chemin du Sud»] voie vers la demeure de <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. «doctrine du Sud», ordre de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a> dont le <a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a> se trouve à <a href="#entry-Uz_rfgeri" class="skt-link">Śṛṅgeri</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_naamuurti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣiṇāmūrti</i> [<a href="#entry-muurti" class="skt-link">mūrti</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_si_naamuurti" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Dakṣiṇāmūrti, aspect de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «célébrant l'offrande», maître de la musique, du <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a> et du savoir; il est représenté sur la face Sud des temples; il accorde le <a href="#entry-svaatmavij_naana" class="skt-link">svātmavijñāna</a>.<br> <i>vīṇādharadakṣiṇāmūrti</i> myth. <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> enseignant la musique et les arts.<br> <i>yogadakṣiṇāmūrti</i> myth. <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> enseignant le <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>.<br> <i>jñānadakṣiṇāmūrti</i> myth. <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> dispensant la connaissance.<br> <i>vyākhyānadakṣiṇāmūrti</i> myth. <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> expliquant les textes sacrés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_naamuurtistotra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣiṇāmūrtistotra</i> [<a href="#entry-stotra" class="skt-link">stotra</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_si_naamuurtistotra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Dakṣiṇāmūrtistotra, hymne à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> en dix stances dû à <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>; <a href="#entry-Usurezvara" class="skt-link">Sureśvara</a> en fit un commentaire [<a href="#entry-vaarttika" class="skt-link">vārttika</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-dak_si_naamuurti" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇāmūrti</a> <br> <a href="#entry-dak_si_naa" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_naapatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣiṇāpatha</i> [<a href="#entry-patha" class="skt-link">patha</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_si_naapatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. la région du Sud, le Deccan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. la route du Sud de l'empire d'<a href="#entry-Uazoka" class="skt-link">Aśoka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_naayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣiṇāyana</i> [<a href="#entry-ayana" class="skt-link">ayana</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_si_naayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. [«le chemin du Sud»] voie vers la demeure de <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. période (défavorable) entre le solstice d'été et le solstice d'hiver (où le soleil paraît se déplacer vers le Sud, et les jours raccourcissent); solstice d'hiver; opp. <a href="#entry-uttaraaya_na" class="skt-link">uttarāyaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_nakaalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣiṇakālī</i> [<a href="#entry-kaalii" class="skt-link">kālī</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_si_nakaalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. du sanctuaire de Dakṣiṇakālī au Népal; il est situé au sud de <a href="#entry-Ukathmandu" class="skt-link">Kathmandu</a>, près des gorges de Chobar; on y sacrifie divers animaux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_nakaazi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣiṇakāśi</i> [<a href="#entry-kaazi" class="skt-link">kāśi</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_si_nakaazi" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Dakṣiṇakāśi, épith. de <a href="#entry-Udraak_saaraama" class="skt-link">Drākṣārāma</a> «Bénarès du Sud».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_natas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣiṇatas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. à droite.<br> <i>dakṣiṇataskaparda</i> qui porte le chignon à droite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dak_si_nezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dakṣiṇeśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-dak_si_nezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du temple Dakṣiṇeśvara, près de Calcutta, au bord du Gange (branche Hūglī), construit en 1855, consacré à <a href="#entry-Ubhava" class="skt-link">Bhava</a>-<a href="#entry-Utaari_nii" class="skt-link">Tāriṇī</a>, aspect de <a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a>; <a href="#entry-Uraamak_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Rāmakṛṣṇa</a> en fut le prêtre.<br> <a href="#entry-dak_si_na" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दकार</span> <i>dakāra</i> [<i>(da)</i>-<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-dakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> le son ou la lettre ‘<i>da</i>’.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dal">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दल्</span> <i>dal</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(dalati)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>daliṣyati</i><i>)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dalita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i><i>)</i> crever, éclater; fendre, s'ouvrir brusquement; s'épanouir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दल</span> <i>dala</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dal" class="skt-link">dal</a>] <a href="#entry-dala" class="skt-link">n.</a> pétale, feuille; bourgeon, jeune pousse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fragment, morceau, portion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dalana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दलन</span> <i>dalana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-dal" class="skt-link">dal</a>] <a href="#entry-dalana" class="skt-link">n.</a> éclatement, rupture <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> action de fendre, de faire éclater, de rompre; destruction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dalita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दलित</span> <i>dalita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-dal" class="skt-link">dal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dalita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dalita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dalitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dalitā</i> fendu, éclaté; ouvert, épanoui (bouton), éclaté (bourgeon); déplié, déployé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> brisé, broyé; dispersé, pulvérisé, détruit, mis en pièces <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en poudre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. opprimé; qualifie les intouchables sans référence à leur caste (Ambedkar).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dam_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दम्</span> <i>dam_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(dāmyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dānta</i><i>)</i> contrôler, apprivoiser, discipliner, dompter; maîtriser, soumettre, punir — ca. <i>(damayati)</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. δαμνημι; lat. <i>domo</i>; ang. to tame; all. zähmen; fr. dompter.<br> <i>dāmyata</i> <i>datta</i> <i>dayadhvam</i> [BU.] Disciplinez vous, soyez charitables, compatissez.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dam_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दम्</span> <i>dam_2</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-dam_1" class="skt-link">dam_1</a>] <a href="#entry-dam_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. maison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दम</span> <i>dama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-dam_1" class="skt-link">dam_1</a>] <a href="#entry-dama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dama" class="skt-link">n.</a> maison, demeure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> maîtrise de soi, empire sur soi-même, contrôle de ses passions <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> discipline, fermeté; domination, domptage; punition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] l'ascèse, comprenant l'abstinence, l'une des 6 vertus cardinales [<a href="#entry-_sa_tsampad" class="skt-link">ṣaṭsampad</a>] — ifc. qui dompte, qui maîtrise, qui contrôle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. δομος; lat. <i>domus</i>; ang. to tame; all. zähmen; fr. dompter, domaine, domicile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-damagho_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>damaghoṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-gho_sa" class="skt-link">ghoṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-damagho_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Damaghoṣa, prince des <a href="#entry-Ucedayas" class="skt-link">Cedayas</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Uzizupaala" class="skt-link">Śiśupāla</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-damagho_saja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>damaghoṣaja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] <a href="#entry-damagho_saja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Damaghoṣaja, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uzizupaala" class="skt-link">Śiśupāla</a> «né de <a href="#entry-Udamagho_sa" class="skt-link">Damaghoṣa</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-damagho_sasuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>damaghoṣasuta</i> [<a href="#entry-suta_2" class="skt-link">suta_2</a>] <a href="#entry-damagho_sasuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Damaghoṣasuta, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uzizupaala" class="skt-link">Śiśupāla</a> «né de <a href="#entry-Udamagho_sa" class="skt-link">Damaghoṣa</a>».<br> <a href="#entry-damagho_sa" class="skt-link">damaghoṣa</a> <br> <a href="#entry-dama" class="skt-link">dama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-damana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दमन</span> <i>damana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dam_1" class="skt-link">dam_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-damana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-damana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-damanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>damanī</i> qui réprime, qui soumet, qui l'emporte — <a href="#entry-damana" class="skt-link">m.</a> dompteur, cocher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. bot. <i>Artemisia vulgaris</i>, armoise; elle est utilisée en médecine, pour contrôler les humeurs <a href="#entry-vaata" class="skt-link">vāta</a> et <a href="#entry-kapha" class="skt-link">kapha</a>; elle est sacrée à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> au Népal — <a href="#entry-damana" class="skt-link">n.</a> discipline; domptage; répression, punition.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-damanaaropa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>damanāropaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-aaropa_na" class="skt-link">āropaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-damanaaropa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. np. d'une cérémonie d'initiation, le 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-caitra" class="skt-link">caitra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-damana" class="skt-link">damana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-damanaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>damanaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-damanaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [PT.] np. du chacal Damanaka «Manipulateur», conseiller du roi-lion <a href="#entry-Upifgalaka_1" class="skt-link">Piṅgalaka_1</a>; sa mauvaise influence prévaut sur les sages conseils de son frère <a href="#entry-Ukara_taka" class="skt-link">Karaṭaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-damayat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दमयत्</span> <i>damayat</i> [ca. ppr. <a href="#entry-dam_1" class="skt-link">dam_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-damayat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-damayat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-damayantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>damayantī</i> qui dompte (not. ses passions); qui domine — <a href="#entry-damayat" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Damayantī «Qui domine (en beauté toutes ses rivales)» fille du roi <a href="#entry-Ubhiima_1" class="skt-link">Bhīma_1</a> du pays <a href="#entry-Uvidarbha" class="skt-link">Vidarbha</a>, d'une très grande beauté; elle épousa le roi <a href="#entry-Unala" class="skt-link">Nala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dambh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दम्भ्</span> <i>dambh</i> cf. <a href="#entry-dabh" class="skt-link">dabh</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dambha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दम्भ</span> <i>dambha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-dabh" class="skt-link">dabh</a>] <a href="#entry-dambha" class="skt-link">m.</a> tromperie, fraude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hypocrisie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Dambha «Hypocrite», personnifiant la tromperie, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uadharma" class="skt-link">Adharma</a> et de <a href="#entry-Um_r_saa" class="skt-link">Mṛṣā</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Ulobha" class="skt-link">Lobha</a> par <a href="#entry-Upu_s_ti" class="skt-link">Puṣṭi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dampati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dampati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-dampati" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. <i>dampatī</i> [«les deux maîtres de maison»] le couple, mari et femme.<br> <a href="#entry-dam_2" class="skt-link">dam_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-danaayu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दनायु</span> <i>danāyu</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-danu" class="skt-link">danu</a>] <a href="#entry-danaayu" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [véd.] np. de Danāyu, fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>; elle eut comme fils de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> les démons [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] <a href="#entry-Uvala" class="skt-link">Vala</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvik_sara_1" class="skt-link">Vikṣara_1</a>, <a href="#entry-Uviira" class="skt-link">Vīra</a> et <a href="#entry-Uv_rtra" class="skt-link">Vṛtra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-danta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दन्त</span> <i>danta</i> var. <i>dat</i> <a href="#entry-danta" class="skt-link">m.</a> dent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> défense d'éléphant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 32 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>dens</i>; ang. tooth; all. zahn; fr. dent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dantadhaavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dantadhāvana</i> [<a href="#entry-dhaavana_2" class="skt-link">dhāvana_2</a>] <a href="#entry-dantadhaavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> brossage des dents.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dantakaa_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dantakāṣṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-kaa_s_tha" class="skt-link">kāṣṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-dantakaa_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> cure-dents.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dantakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dantakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-dantakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> artisan de l'ivoire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dantapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dantapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-dantapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville sainte bd. Dantapura «ville de la dent (relique du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>)», ancienne capitale du <a href="#entry-Ukalifga" class="skt-link">Kaliṅga</a>; mod. <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dantavakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dantavakra</i> [<a href="#entry-vakra" class="skt-link">vakra</a>] <a href="#entry-dantavakra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du <a href="#entry-daanava" class="skt-link">dānava</a> Dantavakra, roi des <a href="#entry-karuu_saas" class="skt-link">karūṣās</a>; il s'opposa à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> qui le tua.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dantavii_naa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dantavīṇā</i> [<a href="#entry-vii_naa" class="skt-link">vīṇā</a>] <a href="#entry-dantavii_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> bruit fait en claquant des dents.<br> <i>dantavīṇām</i> <i>vād</i> claquer des dents.<br> <a href="#entry-danta" class="skt-link">danta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dantya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दन्त्य</span> <i>dantya</i> [<a href="#entry-danta" class="skt-link">danta</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dantya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dantya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dantyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dantyā</i> dental — <a href="#entry-dantya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. phonème dental.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-danu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दनु</span> <i>danu</i> [<a href="#entry-daanava" class="skt-link">dānava</a>] <a href="#entry-danu" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Danu ou Danū «Naïade», fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, mère des démons ondins [<a href="#entry-daanavaas" class="skt-link">dānavās</a>] par <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a>; c'est une divinité des eaux; elle est aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Udanaayu" class="skt-link">Danāyu</a>, et on la dit not. mère de <a href="#entry-Uv_rtra" class="skt-link">Vṛtra</a> — <a href="#entry-danu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Danu_1, épith. du <a href="#entry-daanava" class="skt-link">dānava</a> <a href="#entry-Uvizvaavasu" class="skt-link">Viśvāvasu</a>-<a href="#entry-Ukabandha" class="skt-link">Kabandha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर</span> <i>dara</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-d_rr" class="skt-link">dṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>darā</i> ifc. qui déchire, qui fend, qui détruit — <a href="#entry-dara" class="skt-link">m.</a> cavité, cave, caverne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conque; nombril <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> crainte — <a href="#entry-darii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>darī</i> cavité, caverne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दरद</span> <i>darada</i> <a href="#entry-darada" class="skt-link">n.</a> vermillon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darbha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्भ</span> <i>darbha</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-d_rbh" class="skt-link">dṛbh</a>] <a href="#entry-darbha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. véd. gerbe d'herbe <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a> employée dans le rituel; cf. <a href="#entry-prastara" class="skt-link">prastara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darbhavaaha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>darbhavāhaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-vaahana" class="skt-link">vāhana</a>] <a href="#entry-darbhavaaha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> véd. charrette d'herbe.<br> <a href="#entry-darbha" class="skt-link">darbha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daridr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दरिद्र्</span> <i>daridr</i> [int. <a href="#entry-draa_1" class="skt-link">drā_1</a>] int. <i>(daridrāti)</i> être pauvre [errer].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daridra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दरिद्र</span> <i>daridra</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-daridr" class="skt-link">daridr</a>] a. <a href="#entry-daridra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daridra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-daridraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daridrā</i> pauvre, nécessiteux — <a href="#entry-daridra" class="skt-link">m.</a> miséreux; vagabond [errant].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daridrataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daridratā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-daridrataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> indigence, pénurie; fait d'être privé de ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daridratva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daridratva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-daridratva" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-daridra" class="skt-link">daridra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darp">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्प्</span> <i>darp</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-d_rp" class="skt-link">dṛp</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(darpayati)</i> affoler, rendre orgueilleux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्प</span> <i>darpa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-d_rp" class="skt-link">dṛp</a>] <a href="#entry-darpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> orgueil, vanité; arrogance, folie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Darpa l'Orgueil personnifié, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uadharma" class="skt-link">Adharma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darpa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्पण</span> <i>darpaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-darp" class="skt-link">darp</a>] <a href="#entry-darpa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> miroir [«qui rend vain»] — ifc. lit. forme des titres d'ouvrages [«réflexion sur»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darpaadhmaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>darpādhmāta</i> [<a href="#entry-aadhmaata" class="skt-link">ādhmāta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-darpaadhmaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-darpaadhmaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-darpaadhmaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>darpādhmātā</i> bouffi d'orgueil.<br> <a href="#entry-darpa" class="skt-link">darpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darvi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्वि</span> <i>darvi</i> [<a href="#entry-dru_3" class="skt-link">dru_3</a>] <a href="#entry-darvi" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«en bois»] cuiller, spatule.<br> <i>darvi</i> <i>pākarasaṃ</i> <i>yathā</i> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] Comme une cuiller en bois dans la sauce (bien qu'immergée dedans elle en ignore le goût).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्श्</span> <i>darś</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(darśayati)</i> faire voir, montrer — ps. <i>(darśyate)</i> être montré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्श</span> <i>darśa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] <a href="#entry-darza" class="skt-link">m.</a> ifc. vision de; regard sur, contemplation de — <a href="#entry-darza" class="skt-link">m.</a> apparition (not. de la nouvelle lune) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. nouvelle lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. rite célébré à la nouvelle lune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darzaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्शक</span> <i>darśaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-darzaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-darzaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-darzikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>darśikā</i> qui voit, qui regarde <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui montre, qui éclaircit — <a href="#entry-darzaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> spectateur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [KSs.] np. du roi Darśaka du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Upadmaavatii_2" class="skt-link">Padmāvatī_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darzana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्शन</span> <i>darśana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] <a href="#entry-darzana" class="skt-link">n.</a> vue, vision; aspect <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> visite; spectacle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. vision de l'idole dans le sanctuaire d'un temple <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. méthode, point de vue doctrinal, école de pensée, système philosophique, doctrine de salut; cf. <a href="#entry-_sa_ddarzana" class="skt-link">ṣaḍdarśana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. grande boucle d'oreille plate identifiant un <a href="#entry-naathayogin" class="skt-link">nāthayogin</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>dassana</i>.<br> <i>cārudarśana</i> d'aspect plaisant.<br> <i>punar</i> <i>darśanāya</i> (adr.) au revoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darzaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्शनीय</span> <i>darśanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-darzaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-darzaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-darzaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>darśanīyā</i> visible; qu'on doit voir, qui mérite d'être vu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> agréable à voir, plaisant, joli, beau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darzapuur_namaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>darśapūrṇamāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-puur_namaasa" class="skt-link">pūrṇamāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-darzapuur_namaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. <i>pūrṇamāsau</i> la nouvelle lune et la pleine lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. cérémonies de nouvelle et pleine lune; [ChU.] le beurre clarifié y est versé dans le feu <a href="#entry-dak_si_naagni" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇāgni</a> pour servir d'aliment à la Lune.<br> <a href="#entry-darza" class="skt-link">darśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darzata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्शत</span> <i>darśata</i> [<a href="#entry-darza" class="skt-link">darśa</a><a href="#entry--ta" class="skt-link">-ta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-darzata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-darzata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-darzataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>darśatā</i> visible; à voir; digne d'être vu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-darzin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दर्शिन्</span> <i>darśin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-darzin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-darzin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-darzinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>darśinī</i> qui voit, qui regarde, qui observe, qui examine; qui comprend, qui sait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-das">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दस्</span> <i>das</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(dasyati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(dāsati)</i> pft. <i>(dadāsa)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <i>(adasat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dasta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> être en manque de; être épuisé — ca. <i>(dāsayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dāsita</i><i>)</i> épuiser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dasra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दस्र</span> <i>dasra</i> <a href="#entry-dasra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. véd. np. de Dasra, un <a href="#entry-azvin" class="skt-link">aśvin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dasta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दस्त</span> <i>dasta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-das" class="skt-link">das</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dasta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dasta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dastaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dastā</i> épuisé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dasyu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दस्यु</span> <i>dasyu</i> [<a href="#entry-das" class="skt-link">das</a><a href="#entry--yu" class="skt-link">-yu</a>] <a href="#entry-dasyu" class="skt-link">m.</a> ennemi des dieux, impie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sauvage, barbare; voleur, brigand, scélérat; hors-caste, manant; syn. <a href="#entry-daasa" class="skt-link">dāsa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दत्</span> <i>dat</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-danta" class="skt-link">danta</a>] <a href="#entry-dat" class="skt-link">m.</a> dent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-datta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दत्त</span> <i>datta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dattā</i> donné, accordé, fourni, offert; reçu en donation — <a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">n.</a> don, donation, cadeau — ifc. donné par <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> donné à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a reçu, doué de; forme des np. not. de <a href="#entry-vaizya" class="skt-link">vaiśya</a> — iic. auquel on a donné.<br> <i>dattavadhyacihna</i> qui porte la marque du condamné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dattaatreya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dattātreya</i> [<a href="#entry-aatreya" class="skt-link">ātreya</a>] <a href="#entry-dattaatreya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [BhP.] np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Dattātreya «Issu d'<a href="#entry-Uatri" class="skt-link">Atri</a>», fils d'<a href="#entry-Uanasuuyaa" class="skt-link">Anasūyā</a> comme incarnation [<a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; il devint ermite dès son plus jeune âge près de la <a href="#entry-Unarmadaa" class="skt-link">Narmadā</a>; il accorda à <a href="#entry-Uarjuna_1" class="skt-link">Arjuna_1</a> <a href="#entry-Ukaartaviirya" class="skt-link">Kārtavīrya</a> 1000 bras, et la jeunesse éternelle pour défendre le <a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a>; il est considéré comme le patron des lettrés [<a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a>]; on l'appelle aussi Guru Datta, et il est célébré à <a href="#entry-gurudvaadazii" class="skt-link">gurudvādaśī</a>; on lui attribue l'<a href="#entry-Uavadhuutagiitaa" class="skt-link">Avadhūtagītā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">datta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dattvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दत्त्वा</span> <i>dattvā</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] ind. ayant donné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dauhitra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दौहित्र</span> <i>dauhitra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-duhit_r" class="skt-link">duhitṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-dauhitra" class="skt-link">m.</a> petit-fils (fils d'une fille) — <a href="#entry-dauhitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dauhitrī</i> petite-fille (fille d'une fille).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dauraatmya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दौरात्म्य</span> <i>daurātmya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-duraatman" class="skt-link">durātman</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-dauraatmya" class="skt-link">n.</a> méchanceté, dûreté; dépravation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daurbalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दौर्बल्य</span> <i>daurbalya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-durbala" class="skt-link">durbala</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-daurbalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> faiblesse; incapacité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-davathu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दवथु</span> <i>davathu</i> [<a href="#entry-du" class="skt-link">du</a><a href="#entry--athu" class="skt-link">-athu</a>] <a href="#entry-davathu" class="skt-link">m.</a> tourment, vexation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-day">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दय्</span> <i>day</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(dayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dayita</i><i>)</i> compatir; partager; avoir pitié de, éprouver de la compassion pour ⟨acc. g.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éprouver de la sympathie ou de l'affection pour ⟨acc.⟩; aimer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. δαιοω.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dayaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दया</span> <i>dayā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-day" class="skt-link">day</a>] <a href="#entry-dayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> compassion, pitié; pitié envers ⟨loc. g. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [BhP.] np. de Dayā «Pitié», fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, une des épouses de <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a>.<br> <i>dayāṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> prendre pitié de ⟨loc. g.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dayaalu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दयालु</span> <i>dayālu</i> [<a href="#entry-dayaa" class="skt-link">dayā</a><a href="#entry--lu" class="skt-link">-lu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dayaalu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dayaalu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dayaalu" class="skt-link">f.</a> compatissant, sympathisant, gentil.<br> <i>hare</i> <i>dayālo</i> <i>naḥ</i> <i>pāhi</i> [SK.] Ô, gentil <a href="#entry-Uhari" class="skt-link">Hari</a>, protège-nous.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dayaananda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dayānanda</i> [<a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">ānanda</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dayaananda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dayaananda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dayaanandaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dayānandā</i> qui éprouve la joie de la compassion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Dayānanda «Joie de compassion», nom de renonçant; mod. not. le <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a> Dayānanda_1 <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_2</a> (1824–1883), né au Gujarāt, qui fonda le mouvement de renouveau hindou <a href="#entry-Uaaryaasamaaja" class="skt-link">Āryāsamāja</a> en 1875; il est l'auteur not. du <a href="#entry-Usatyaarthaprakaaza" class="skt-link">Satyārthaprakāśa</a>; son maître était le <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a> <a href="#entry-Uvirajaananda" class="skt-link">Virajānanda</a> <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dayaavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dayāvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dayaavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dayaavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dayaavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dayāvatī</i> compatissant, miséricordieux.<br> <a href="#entry-dayaa" class="skt-link">dayā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dayita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दयित</span> <i>dayita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-day" class="skt-link">day</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dayita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dayita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dayitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dayitā</i> aimé, bien-aimé, chéri — <a href="#entry-dayita" class="skt-link">m.</a> bien-aimé, époux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-daza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दश</span> <i>daśa</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-dazan" class="skt-link">daśan</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दशा</span> <i>daśā</i> [<a href="#entry-da_mz" class="skt-link">daṃś</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> frange de fils de trame; mèche de lampe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (au fig.) condition, état, destin; circonstance, âge de la vie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. configuration de naissance en astrologie prédictive [<a href="#entry-jyoti_sa" class="skt-link">jyotiṣa</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-ulkaa" class="skt-link">ulkā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśāha</i> [<a href="#entry-aha" class="skt-link">aha</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> dix jours <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. (10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour) festival de victoire de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> sur <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>; il clôt le festival de <a href="#entry-durgaapuujaa" class="skt-link">durgāpūjā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-navaraatra" class="skt-link">navarātra</a>, <a href="#entry-vijayadazamii" class="skt-link">vijayadaśamī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>daśāra</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaak_sara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśākṣara</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sara" class="skt-link">akṣara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dazaak_sara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dazaak_sara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dazaak_sarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśākṣarī</i> constitué de 10 syllables.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaak_saramantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśākṣaramantra</i> [<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaak_saramantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> de 10 syllabes utilisé not. pour le <a href="#entry-vibhuutipa_njaranyaasa" class="skt-link">vibhūtipañjaranyāsa</a> (<i>gopījanavallabhāya</i> <a href="#entry-svaahaa" class="skt-link">svāhā</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-dazaak_sara" class="skt-link">daśākṣara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaanana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśānana</i> [<a href="#entry-aanana" class="skt-link">ānana</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaanana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Daśānana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> «aux Dix bouches».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaapavitra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśāpavitra</i> [<a href="#entry-pavitra" class="skt-link">pavitra</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaapavitra" class="skt-link">n.</a> véd. filtre en laine.<br> <i>daśāpavitreṇa</i> <i>grahaṃ</i> <i>saṃmārṣṭi</i> On essuie la cuiller avec un filtre en laine.<br> <a href="#entry-dazaa" class="skt-link">daśā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśārṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ar_na" class="skt-link">arṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. géo. [«Dix lacs»] np. du pays et du peuple des daśārṇās, au Sud-Est du <a href="#entry-Umadhyadeza" class="skt-link">Madhyadeśa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uhira_nyavarmaa" class="skt-link">Hiraṇyavarmā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaavataara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśāvatāra</i> [<a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaavataara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> «aux dix avatars» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>daśāvatārās</i> myth. les dix incarnations de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>: <a href="#entry-Umatsya" class="skt-link">Matsya</a> le Poisson sauveur du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukuurma" class="skt-link">Kūrma</a> la Tortue socle du monde, <a href="#entry-Uvaraaha" class="skt-link">Varāha</a> le Sanglier sauveur de la Terre, <a href="#entry-Unarasi_mha" class="skt-link">Narasiṃha</a> l'Homme-lion qui détruit l'injustice, <a href="#entry-Uvaamana" class="skt-link">Vāmana</a>-<a href="#entry-Utrivikrama" class="skt-link">Trivikrama</a> le Nain aux trois enjambées cosmiques, <a href="#entry-Uparazuraama" class="skt-link">Paraśurāma</a> le Brâhmane à la Hache, <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> le Roi Charmant, <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> le Prince Noir, <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a> le Prince Blanc et <a href="#entry-Ukalkii" class="skt-link">Kalkī</a> le Cavalier de l'Apocalypse; parfois on remplace <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a> par <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. du temple Daśāvatāra «des dix avatars» (de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>), de période <a href="#entry-Ugupta" class="skt-link">Gupta</a> (5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), de style <a href="#entry-pa_ncaayatana" class="skt-link">pañcāyatana</a>; il se trouve au village fortifié de Deogarh (au sud du mod. Uttar Pradesh).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaazvamedha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśāśvamedha</i> [<a href="#entry-azvamedha" class="skt-link">aśvamedha</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaazvamedha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du quai [<a href="#entry-gha_t_ta" class="skt-link">ghaṭṭa</a>] Daśāśvamedha sur le Gange à Bénarès, ainsi nommé car le bain à cet endroit vaut «10 sacrifices de cheval».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazagiitikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśagītikā</i> [<a href="#entry-giitikaa" class="skt-link">gītikā</a>] <a href="#entry-dazagiitikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Daśagītikā, section de l'<a href="#entry-Uaaryabha_tiiya" class="skt-link">Āryabhaṭīya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazagrantha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśagrantha</i> [<a href="#entry-grantha" class="skt-link">grantha</a>] <a href="#entry-dazagrantha" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. lit. les dix livres essentiels au <a href="#entry-_rgvedin" class="skt-link">ṛgvedin</a>: <a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>, <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-aara_nyaka" class="skt-link">āraṇyaka</a>, <a href="#entry-zik_saa" class="skt-link">śikṣā</a>, <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>, <a href="#entry-vyaakara_na" class="skt-link">vyākaraṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-nigha_n_tu" class="skt-link">nighaṇṭu</a>, <a href="#entry-nirukta" class="skt-link">nirukta</a>, <a href="#entry-chandas" class="skt-link">chandas</a> et <a href="#entry-jyoti_sa" class="skt-link">jyotiṣa</a>; ou bien les 4 <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> et les 6 <a href="#entry-vedaafga" class="skt-link">vedāṅga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazagranthin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśagranthin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dazagranthin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dazagranthin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dazagranthinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśagranthinī</i> soc. se dit d'un brâhmane qui a étudié les 4 Savoirs [<a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>] et les 6 disciplines annexes [<a href="#entry-vedaafga" class="skt-link">vedāṅga</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-dazagrantha" class="skt-link">daśagrantha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazagriiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśagrīva</i> [<a href="#entry-griivaa" class="skt-link">grīvā</a>] <a href="#entry-dazagriiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Daśagrīva, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> «aux dix têtes».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaharaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśaharā</i> [<a href="#entry-haraa" class="skt-link">harā</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. [«celle qui enlève les 10 péchés»] épith. de <a href="#entry-Ugafgaa" class="skt-link">Gaṅgā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. festival en honneur de la <a href="#entry-Ugafgaa" class="skt-link">Gaṅgā</a>, le 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-jyai_s_tha" class="skt-link">jyaiṣṭha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. np. du festival de Daśaharā en l'honneur de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>, au mois <a href="#entry-aazvina" class="skt-link">āśvina</a> (mod. Dassera); cf. <a href="#entry-navaraatra" class="skt-link">navarātra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśaika</i> [<a href="#entry-eka" class="skt-link">eka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dazaika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dazaika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dazaikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśaikā</i> pl. onze.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaikaadaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśaikādaśa</i> [<a href="#entry-ekaadazan" class="skt-link">ekādaśan</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaikaadaza" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«onze à la dizaine»] (prêt) qui rapporte à dix pour cent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaikaadazika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśaikādaśika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dazaikaadazika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dazaikaadazika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dazaikaadazikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśaikādaśikī</i> [«qui prête à dix pour cent»] usurier.<br> <a href="#entry-dazaikaadaza" class="skt-link">daśaikādaśa</a> <br> <a href="#entry-daza" class="skt-link">daśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dazaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dazaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dazikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśikā</i> consistant en 10 composantes — <a href="#entry-dazaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> dizaine, décade; catégorie de 10 objets <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. not. poème en 10 vers <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du Daśaka, attribué au sage <a href="#entry-Uvyaaghrapaada" class="skt-link">Vyāghrapāda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaka_n_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśakaṇṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kaṇṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaka_n_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Daśakaṇṭha, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> «aux dix gorges» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> thai thoskonth.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazakumaaracarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśakumāracarita</i> [<a href="#entry-kumaara" class="skt-link">kumāra</a>-<a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">carita</a>] <a href="#entry-dazakumaaracarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Daśakumāracarita «Aventures des dix princes», roman d'aventures et d'émancipation de <a href="#entry-Uda_n_dii_1" class="skt-link">Daṇḍī_1</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uraajavaahana" class="skt-link">Rājavāhana</a>, <a href="#entry-Uarthapaala" class="skt-link">Arthapāla</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśama</i> [<a href="#entry--ma" class="skt-link">-ma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dazama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dazama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dazamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśamī</i> ord. dixième — <a href="#entry-dazamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśamī</i> astr. le 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour d'une quinzaine lunaire; cf. <a href="#entry-vijayadazamii" class="skt-link">vijayadaśamī</a>, <a href="#entry-saambadazamii" class="skt-link">sāmbadaśamī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazamaa_mza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśamāṃśa</i> [<a href="#entry-a_mza" class="skt-link">aṃśa</a>] <a href="#entry-dazamaa_mza" class="skt-link">m.</a> dîme.<br> <a href="#entry-dazama" class="skt-link">daśama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazamahaavidyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśamahāvidyā</i> [<a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">mahāvidyā</a>] <a href="#entry-dazamahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>] les dix aspects terribles ou pouvoirs cosmiques de la Déesse [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>]: <a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a>-<a href="#entry-Ukaalikaa" class="skt-link">Kālikā</a>, <a href="#entry-Utaaraa" class="skt-link">Tārā</a>, <a href="#entry-U_so_dazii" class="skt-link">Ṣoḍaśī</a> (ou <a href="#entry-Uraajaraajezvarii" class="skt-link">Rājarājeśvarī</a>), <a href="#entry-Ubhuvanezvarii" class="skt-link">Bhuvaneśvarī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubhairavii" class="skt-link">Bhairavī</a>, <a href="#entry-Uchinnamastaa" class="skt-link">Chinnamastā</a> (ou <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dii" class="skt-link">Caṇḍī</a>-<a href="#entry-Uca_n_dikaa" class="skt-link">Caṇḍikā</a>), <a href="#entry-Udhuumavatii" class="skt-link">Dhūmavatī</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvagalaa" class="skt-link">Vagalā</a>, <a href="#entry-Umaatafgii" class="skt-link">Mātaṅgī</a>, et <a href="#entry-Ukamalaa" class="skt-link">Kamalā</a>-<a href="#entry-Usiddhilak_smii" class="skt-link">Siddhilakṣmī</a>; parfois on liste aussi <a href="#entry-Ucaamu_n_dii" class="skt-link">Cāmuṇḍī</a>, et <a href="#entry-Utripurasundarii" class="skt-link">Tripurasundarī</a>-<a href="#entry-Ulalitaa" class="skt-link">Lalitā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Daśamahāvidyā, épith. de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> «aux dix grands pouvoirs».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazamuula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśamūla</i> [<a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a>] <a href="#entry-dazamuula" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. préparation tonique mélangeant les 5 grandes racines [<a href="#entry-b_rhatpa_ncamuula" class="skt-link">bṛhatpañcamūla</a>] et les 5 petites racines [<a href="#entry-hrasvapa_ncamuula" class="skt-link">hrasvapañcamūla</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दशन्</span> <i>daśan</i> num. <a href="#entry-dazan" class="skt-link">*</a> le nombre dix — a. <a href="#entry-daza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-daza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>daśa</i> <a href="#entry-dazaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśā</i> ifc. dizième <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. δεκα; lat. <i>decem</i>; ang. ten; all. zehn; fr. dix.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दशन</span> <i>daśana</i> [<a href="#entry-da_mz" class="skt-link">daṃś</a>] <a href="#entry-dazana" class="skt-link">m.</a> dent; dentition.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazanaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśanāma</i> [<a href="#entry-naama_2" class="skt-link">nāma_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dazanaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dazanaama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dazanaamnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśanāmnī</i> qui a dix noms.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazanaamiida_n_din">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśanāmīdaṇḍin</i> [<a href="#entry-da_n_din" class="skt-link">daṇḍin</a>] <a href="#entry-dazanaamiida_n_din" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. ordre mendiant de la doctrine <a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> fondé par <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-dazanaama" class="skt-link">daśanāma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazanavasanaafgaraaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśanavasanāṅgarāga</i> [<a href="#entry-vasana_2" class="skt-link">vasana_2</a>-<a href="#entry-afgaraaga" class="skt-link">aṅgarāga</a>] <a href="#entry-dazanavasanaafgaraaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> la coloration des dents et des vêtements, un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-dazana" class="skt-link">daśana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaraatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśarātra</i> [<a href="#entry-raatra" class="skt-link">rātra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dazaraatra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dazaraatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dazaraatraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśarātrā</i> qui dure dix jours — <a href="#entry-dazaraatra" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. période de dix jours terminant l'année véd. [<a href="#entry-gavaamayana" class="skt-link">gavāmayana</a>]; le dernier jour, on célèbre le <a href="#entry-mahaavrata" class="skt-link">mahāvrata</a>; la dernière nuit en est l'<a href="#entry-atiraatra" class="skt-link">atirātra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaratha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśaratha</i> [<a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaratha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Daśaratha «au Char victorieux dans les dix directions», roi du <a href="#entry-Ukosala" class="skt-link">Kosala</a>, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uaja" class="skt-link">Aja</a> de la lignée solaire [<a href="#entry-suuryava_mza" class="skt-link">sūryavaṃśa</a>]; à l'âge de 60000 ans, malgré ses 3 épouses, il n'avait pas d'héritier; pour sa conduite vertueuse il reçut une bouillie fécondante qu'il partagea entre ses épouses; sa première épouse <a href="#entry-Ukausalyaa" class="skt-link">Kausalyā</a> en reçut la moitié, et donna naissance à l'aîné <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>, incarnation [<a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; sa seconde épouse <a href="#entry-Usumitraa" class="skt-link">Sumitrā</a>, qui en reçut un quart et un huitième, donna naissance aux jumeaux <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Uzatrughna" class="skt-link">Śatrughna</a>; sa troisième et favorite épouse <a href="#entry-Ukaikeyii" class="skt-link">Kaikeyī</a>, qui en reçut un huitième, donna naissance au cadet <a href="#entry-Ubharata_1" class="skt-link">Bharata_1</a>; sur ses vieux jours il fit de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> son héritier; mais sa favorite <a href="#entry-Ukaikeyii" class="skt-link">Kaikeyī</a>, lui rappelant sa promesse ancienne d'exhaucer un vœu, l'obligea à léguer le royaume à son fils <a href="#entry-Ubharata_1" class="skt-link">Bharata_1</a>, qui régnera au nom de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>; <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> étant parti en exil avec ses frères jumeaux et son épouse <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a>, <a href="#entry-Udazaratha" class="skt-link">Daśaratha</a> mourut de chagrin [<a href="#entry-putrazoka" class="skt-link">putraśoka</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Uzrava_nakumaara" class="skt-link">Śravaṇakumāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaruupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśarūpa</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dazaruupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dazaruupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dazaruupaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśarūpā</i> qui a 10 aspects <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. les 10 aspects de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; par ext., épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> «aux dix aspects» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. les 10 genres principaux de composition littéraire; cf. <a href="#entry-Udazaruupaka" class="skt-link">Daśarūpaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaruupaavaloka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśarūpāvaloka</i> [<a href="#entry-avaloka" class="skt-link">avaloka</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaruupaavaloka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Daśarūpāvaloka «Analyse des dix genres dramatiques»; cf. <a href="#entry-Udazaruupaka" class="skt-link">Daśarūpaka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-dazaruupa" class="skt-link">daśarūpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazaruupaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśarūpaka</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupaka" class="skt-link">rūpaka</a>] <a href="#entry-dazaruupaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Daśarūpaka «Les dix structures dramatiques», ouvrage de rhétorique de <a href="#entry-Udhana_njaya_1" class="skt-link">Dhanañjaya_1</a>; il discute de la composition dramatique et de la structure des récits, not. de la théorie des 5 articulations [<a href="#entry-pa_ncasandhi" class="skt-link">pañcasandhi</a>]; aussi appelé Daśarūpāvaloka.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दशत्</span> <i>daśat</i> [<a href="#entry-dazan" class="skt-link">daśan</a>] <a href="#entry-dazat" class="skt-link">f.</a> dizaine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazataya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>daśataya</i> [<a href="#entry--taya" class="skt-link">-taya</a>] <a href="#entry-dazataya" class="skt-link">n.</a> dizaine.<br> <a href="#entry-dazagriiva" class="skt-link">daśagrīva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dazin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दशिन्</span> <i>daśin</i> [<a href="#entry-daza" class="skt-link">daśa</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dazin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dazin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dazinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>daśinī</i> constitué de 10 parties.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-de_s_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">देष्ण</span> <i>deṣṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-de_s_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> don.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-de_s_t_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">देष्टृ</span> <i>deṣṭṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-diz_1" class="skt-link">diś_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-de_s_t_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-de_s_t_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-de_s_trii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>deṣṭrī</i> indicateur, pointeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-de_s_tra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">देष्ट्र</span> <i>deṣṭra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-diz_1" class="skt-link">diś_1</a>] <a href="#entry-de_s_tra" class="skt-link">n.</a> indication, direction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-deha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">देह</span> <i>deha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dih" class="skt-link">dih</a>] <a href="#entry-deha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-deha" class="skt-link">n.</a> corps; forme; masse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. la forme charnelle de l'homme; opp. ce qui l'anime [<a href="#entry-dehin" class="skt-link">dehin</a>] — ifc. ayant l'apparence de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dehavedha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dehavedha</i> [<a href="#entry-vedha" class="skt-link">vedha</a>] <a href="#entry-dehavedha" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. transsubstantiation du corps (simili de <a href="#entry-lohavedha" class="skt-link">lohavedha</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-deha" class="skt-link">deha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dehi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">देहि</span> <i>dehi</i> cf. <a href="#entry-daa_1" class="skt-link">dā_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dehin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">देहिन्</span> <i>dehin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dih" class="skt-link">dih</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dehin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dehin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dehinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dehinī</i> être vivant, homme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [BhG.] [«ce qui anime le corps et lui survit»] âme; syn. <a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dev">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">देव्</span> <i>dev</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(devati)</i> ca. <i>(devayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dyūna</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dev_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">देवृ</span> <i>devṛ</i> cf. <a href="#entry-devara" class="skt-link">devara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-deva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">देव</span> <i>deva</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-div" class="skt-link">div</a>] a. <a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-devii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>devī</i> brillant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> divin — <a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">m.</a> dieu [«être de lumière»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. personnification d'une manifestation de la puissance divine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>devās</i> myth. les dieux; la tradition en donne une liste conventionnelle de 33 [<a href="#entry-trayastri_mza" class="skt-link">trayastriṃśa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> roi, sa majesté — <a href="#entry-devii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>devī</i> déesse; reine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. np. de Devī, la Déesse — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(devayati)</i> adorer, prier, faire ses dévotions <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>divus</i>; fr. divin; avestique daeva.<br> <i>devau</i> le roi et la reine.<br> <i>naradeva</i> [«dieu parmi les hommes»] roi.<br> <i>jayatu</i> <i>devaḥ</i> vive le roi!<br> <i>devagandharvamānuṣoragarakṣasās</i> les dieux, les génies <a href="#entry-gandharvaas" class="skt-link">gandharvās</a>, les hommes, les esprits serpents et les démons <a href="#entry-rak_sasaas" class="skt-link">rakṣasās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-devaa_mza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>devāṃśa</i> [<a href="#entry-a_mza" class="skt-link">aṃśa</a>] <a href="#entry-devaa_mza" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. incarnation partielle d'un dieu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-devaalaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>devālaya</i> [<a href="#entry-aalaya" class="skt-link">ālaya</a>] <a href="#entry-devaalaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> temple [«séjour de dieu»]; arch. sanctuaire principal d'un temple <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>deval</i>; oriya deul.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-devaanaam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">देवानाम्</span> <i>devānām</i> [g. pl. <a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-devaanaampriya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>devānāṃpriya</i> [<a href="#entry-priya" class="skt-link">priya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-devaanaa_mpriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-devaanaa_mpriya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-devaanaa_mpriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>devānāṃpriyā</i> aimé des dieux; au fig. simple d'esprit — <a href="#entry-devaanaa_mpriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Devānāṃpriya, (<a href="#entry-paali" class="skt-link">pāli</a> Devānaṃpiya), épith. du roi <a href="#entry-Uazoka" class="skt-link">Aśoka</a> «Aimé des Dieux».<br> <a href="#entry-devaanaam" class="skt-link">devānām</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-devaapi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">देवापि</span> <i>devāpi</i> <a href="#entry-devaapi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du prince <a href="#entry-kaurava" class="skt-link">kaurava</a> Devāpi «Proche des dieux»; fils aîné du roi <a href="#entry-Upratiipa" class="skt-link">Pratīpa</a>, il renonça au trône pour se faire ermite; on dit qu'il vit encore; son frère <a href="#entry-Uzaantanu" class="skt-link">Śāṃtanu</a> devint roi à sa place.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-devaavasura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>devāvasura</i> [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] <a href="#entry-devaavasura" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. <i>devāvasurau</i> myth. [RV.] les deux titans devenus des dieux, épith. de <a href="#entry-Umitraavaru_nau" class="skt-link">Mitrāvaruṇau</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-devacarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>devacarita</i> [<a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">carita</a>] <a href="#entry-devacarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> conduite des dieux.<br> <i>na</i> <i>devacaritaṃ</i> <i>caret</i> Ne te comporte pas comme un dieu.
  </div>
</div>
