<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_hzaasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुःशासन</span> <i>duḥśāsana</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-zaasana" class="skt-link">śāsana</a>] <a href="#entry-du_hzaasana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Duḥśāsana «Mauvais conseil», l'un des 100 fils de <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a>; il insulta <a href="#entry-Udraupadii" class="skt-link">Draupadī</a> en défaisant la natte de ses cheveux [<a href="#entry-ve_niisa_mhaara" class="skt-link">veṇīsaṃhāra</a>]; <a href="#entry-Ubhiima" class="skt-link">Bhīma</a> le tua pour réaliser son vœu de boire son sang — <a href="#entry-du_hzaasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> mauvais enseignement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_hzala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुःशल</span> <i>duḥśala</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-zala" class="skt-link">śala</a>] <a href="#entry-du_hzala" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Duḥśala, «Mauvaise lance», l'un des 100 fils de <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a>, tué par <a href="#entry-Ubhiima" class="skt-link">Bhīma</a> — <a href="#entry-du_hzalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duḥśalā</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Duḥśalā, unique fille de <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Ujayadratha" class="skt-link">Jayadratha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_n_dubha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुण्डुभ</span> <i>duṇḍubha</i> <a href="#entry-du_n_dubha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. lézard <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>duṇḍubhi</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_n_duka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुण्डुक</span> <i>duṇḍuka</i> a. <a href="#entry-du_n_duka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-du_n_duka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-du_n_dukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duṇḍukā</i> frauduleux; malveillant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्</span> <i>duṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(duṣyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>duṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> se gâter; être mauvais, corrompu, impur, contaminé, ruiné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pécher, commettre une faute, avoir tort — ca. <i>(dūṣayati)</i> cf. <a href="#entry-duu_s" class="skt-link">dūṣ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्ट</span> <i>duṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-du_s" class="skt-link">duṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-du_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-du_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-du_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duṣṭā</i> gâté, souillé; contaminé, impur, vicié <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> corrompu, dépravé, vicieux; vil, bas, mauvais, méchant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fautif, coupable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> calomnié, avili, insulté — ifc. souillé de — <a href="#entry-du_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> scélérat, criminel; lit. rôle du scélérat au théâtre — <a href="#entry-du_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> péché, crime, souillure; culpabilité; outrage, viol; perversion — <a href="#entry-du_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duṣṭā</i> femme dépravée.<br> <i>dṣṭabaṭuka</i> sale gamin, méchant garçon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_s_taatma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>duṣṭātma</i> [<a href="#entry-aatma" class="skt-link">ātma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-du_s_taatma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-du_s_taatma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-du_s_taatmaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duṣṭātmā</i> à l'âme corrompue; dont la nature est mauvaise.<br> <a href="#entry-du_s_ta" class="skt-link">duṣṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_s_tah_rdaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>duṣṭahṛdaya</i> [<a href="#entry-h_rdaya" class="skt-link">hṛdaya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-du_s_tah_rdaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-du_s_tah_rdaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-du_s_tah_rdayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duṣṭahṛdayā</i> méchant, mauvais, qui a le cœur endurci.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_s_tara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्टर</span> <i>duṣṭara</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-tara" class="skt-link">tara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-du_s_tara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-du_s_tara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-du_s_taraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duṣṭarā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-dustara" class="skt-link">dustara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_s_tataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>duṣṭatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-du_s_tataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> méchanceté, vice; faute, violation, crime.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_s_tatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>duṣṭatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-du_s_tatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> méchanceté, vice; faute, violation, crime.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्टि</span> <i>duṣṭi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-du_s" class="skt-link">duṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-du_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> corruption; souillure; vice <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> infection (blessure).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_sk_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्कृत</span> <i>duṣkṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">kṛta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-du_sk_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-du_sk_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-du_sk_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duṣkṛtā</i> qui agit mal, coupable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mal organisé, mal appliqué, mal fait — <a href="#entry-du_sk_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> mauvaise action, péché; culpabilité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_skara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्कर</span> <i>duṣkara</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-du_skara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-du_skara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-du_skaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duṣkarā</i> difficile à faire, ardu — <a href="#entry-du_skara" class="skt-link">n.</a> difficulté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_skara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्करण</span> <i>duṣkaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-kara_na" class="skt-link">karaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-du_skara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> travail pénible; corvée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_skarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्कर्मन्</span> <i>duṣkarman</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-karman" class="skt-link">karman</a>] <a href="#entry-du_skarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> péché; tâche pénible — a. <a href="#entry-du_skarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-du_skarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-du_skarma_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duṣkarmaṇī</i> méchant, criminel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_skula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्कुल</span> <i>duṣkula</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a>] <a href="#entry-du_skula" class="skt-link">n.</a> mauvaise famille; basse extraction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_smanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्मन्त</span> <i>duṣmanta</i> cf. <a href="#entry-du_syanta" class="skt-link">duṣyanta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_sprayukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्प्रयुक्त</span> <i>duṣprayukta</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-prayukta" class="skt-link">prayukta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-du_sprayukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-du_sprayukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-du_sprayuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duṣprayuktā</i> mal arrangé, en désordre; mésutilisé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_spuura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्पूर</span> <i>duṣpūra</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-puura" class="skt-link">pūra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-du_spuura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-du_spuura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-du_spuuraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duṣpūrā</i> difficile à remplir; difficile à satisfaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-du_syanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुष्यन्त</span> <i>duṣyanta</i> var. <i>duḥṣanta</i>, <i>duṣmanta</i> <a href="#entry-du_syanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Duṣyanta (ou Duḥṣanta ou Duṣmanta), de la lignée lunaire [<a href="#entry-somava_mza" class="skt-link">somavaṃśa</a>], descendant de <a href="#entry-Upuuru" class="skt-link">Pūru</a>, époux de <a href="#entry-Uha_msavatii" class="skt-link">Haṃsavatī</a> puis de <a href="#entry-Uzakuntalaa" class="skt-link">Śakuntalā</a> et père par cette dernière de <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a>; il fut invité au ciel [<a href="#entry-svarga" class="skt-link">svarga</a>] par <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, qui envoya son cocher <a href="#entry-Umaatali" class="skt-link">Mātali</a> le chercher, afin de combattre les démons [<a href="#entry-daanavaas" class="skt-link">dānavās</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. il est le héros du drame <a href="#entry-Uabhij_naanazakuntalaa" class="skt-link">Abhijñānaśakuntalā</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dugdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुग्ध</span> <i>dugdha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-duh_1" class="skt-link">duh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dugdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dugdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dugdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dugdhā</i> trait, tiré (lait) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> extrait, sucé, épuisé, vidé — <a href="#entry-dugdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> lait; suc laiteux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dugdhadoha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dugdhadoha</i> [<a href="#entry-doha" class="skt-link">doha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dugdhadoha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dugdhadoha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dugdhadohaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dugdhadohā</i> trait, dont on a tiré le lait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dugdhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dugdhin</i> [<a href="#entry-dugdha" class="skt-link">dugdha</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dugdhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dugdhin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dugdhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dugdhinī</i> qui a du lait; laiteux — <a href="#entry-dugdhin" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. calcite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dugdhodadhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dugdhodadhi</i> [<a href="#entry-udadhi" class="skt-link">udadhi</a>] <a href="#entry-dugdhodadhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. océan primordial de lait; syn. <a href="#entry-k_siiroda" class="skt-link">kṣīroda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-dugdha" class="skt-link">dugdha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dugha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुघ</span> <i>dugha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-duh_1" class="skt-link">duh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dugha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dugha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dughaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dughā</i> ifc. qui trait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui donne (comme lait); qui accorde — <a href="#entry-dughaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dughā</i> vache laitière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duh_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुह्</span> <i>duh_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(dogdhi)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] <i>(adhukṣat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dugdha</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>duhya</i>, <i>dohya</i>, <i>dohanīya</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(dogdhum)</i> traire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tirer, extraire, exprimer — pr. md. <i>(dugdhe)</i> donner du lait; émettre, faire couler; fournir, accorder — ca. <i>(dohayati)</i> faire traire — dés. <i>(dudhukṣati)</i> vouloir traire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. dug.<br> <i>devā</i> <i>aduhra</i> véd. les dieux accordaient l'abondance.<br> <i>goṣu</i> <i>duhyamānāsu</i> <i>gato</i> <i>devadatto</i> <i>dugdhāsvāgataḥ</i> <a href="#entry-Udevadatta" class="skt-link">Devadatta</a> est parti quand on trayait les vaches et revenu une fois traites.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duh_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुह्</span> <i>duh_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-duh_1" class="skt-link">duh_1</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-duh_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duh_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui trait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui accorde — <a href="#entry-duha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>duha</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duhit_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुहितृ</span> <i>duhitṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-duh_1" class="skt-link">duh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duhit_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duhit_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duhit_r" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui trait — <a href="#entry-duhit_r" class="skt-link">f.</a> fille [«celle qui trait»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. daughter; all. Tochter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुह्य</span> <i>duhya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-duh_1" class="skt-link">duh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duyā</i> à traire, qu'il faut traire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duhyamaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुह्यमान</span> <i>duhyamāna</i> [ppr. ps. <a href="#entry-duh_1" class="skt-link">duh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duhyamaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duhyamaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duhyamaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duhyamānā</i> qui est trait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dukuula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुकूल</span> <i>dukūla</i> <a href="#entry-dukuula" class="skt-link">n.</a> étoffe de soie; mousseline; vêtement fin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dundubha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुन्दुभ</span> <i>dundubha</i> var. <i>duṇḍubha</i> <a href="#entry-dundubha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. serpent d'eau, non venimeux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dundubhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुन्दुभि</span> <i>dundubhi</i> <a href="#entry-dundubhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> mus. grosse caisse — <a href="#entry-dundubhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Dundubhi, <a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>-buffle géant, fils de <a href="#entry-Umaya" class="skt-link">Maya</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uhemaa" class="skt-link">Hemā</a>; <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> lui ayant donné de grands pouvoirs, son orgueil ne connut pas de bornes, et il combattait tous ceux qu'il rencontrait; au bord de l'océan il provoqua <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>, qui l'envoya chez <a href="#entry-Uhimavaan" class="skt-link">Himavān</a>; celui-ci l'envoya au royaume du roi des singes <a href="#entry-Uvaalii" class="skt-link">Vālī</a>, qui le combattit grâce au pouvoir d'un collier d'or que lui avait donné <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; le combat dura pendant une année entière dans la caverne <a href="#entry-Uki_skindhaa" class="skt-link">Kiṣkindhā</a>; lorsque le sang de <a href="#entry-Udundubhi" class="skt-link">Dundubhi</a> jaillit hors de la caverne, <a href="#entry-Usugriiva" class="skt-link">Sugrīva</a>, croyant son frère mort, en mura l'entrée avec une grosse pierre, provoquant la colère de <a href="#entry-Uvaalii" class="skt-link">Vālī</a>; un peu du sang de <a href="#entry-Udundubhi" class="skt-link">Dundubhi</a> retomba sur l'ermitage de <a href="#entry-Umatafga" class="skt-link">Mataṅga</a>, provoquant la colère de ce dernier, qui maudit son responsable de mourir s'il venait à <a href="#entry-U_r_syamuuka" class="skt-link">Ṛṣyamūka</a>; <a href="#entry-Usugriiva" class="skt-link">Sugrīva</a> s'y réfugia <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Dundubhi_1 «Irascible», personnifiant l'an 56 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>] — <a href="#entry-dundubhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dundubhī</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de la <a href="#entry-gandharvii" class="skt-link">gandharvī</a> Dundubhī, compagne de <a href="#entry-Utumburu" class="skt-link">Tumburu</a>; sur les instructions de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> elle s'incarna sur Terre comme la bossue <a href="#entry-Umantharaa" class="skt-link">Mantharā</a> afin d'inciter la jalousie de <a href="#entry-Ukaikeyii" class="skt-link">Kaikeyī</a>, de provoquer l'exil de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>, et finalement de déclencher la guerre qui fit périr <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dundubhilepa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dundubhilepa</i> [<a href="#entry-lepa" class="skt-link">lepa</a>] <a href="#entry-dundubhilepa" class="skt-link">m.</a> pâte à enduire les tambours à fins magiques.<br> <a href="#entry-dundubhi" class="skt-link">dundubhi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dur">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्</span> <i>dur</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dur_naaman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्णामन्</span> <i>durṇāman</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-naaman" class="skt-link">nāman</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dur_naaman" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dur_naaman" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dur_naamnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durṇāmnī</i> qui a un vilain nom.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duraacaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुराचार</span> <i>durācāra</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">ācāra</a>] <a href="#entry-duraacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> mauvaise conduite — a. <a href="#entry-duraacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duraacaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duraacaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durācārā</i> mécréant, méchant; impie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duraagama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुरागम</span> <i>durāgama</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">āgama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duraagama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duraagama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duraagamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durāgamā</i> difficile à approcher; inintelligible — <a href="#entry-duraagama" class="skt-link">m.</a> gain illégitime.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duraasada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुरासद</span> <i>durāsada</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-aasad" class="skt-link">āsad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duraasada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duraasada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duraasadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durāsadā</i> [«difficile à rencontrer»] inouï.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duraatman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुरात्मन्</span> <i>durātman</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duraatman" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duraatman" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duraatmanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durātmanī</i> [«de mauvaise nature»] mauvais, méchant, criminel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duratikrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुरतिक्रम</span> <i>duratikrama</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-atikrama" class="skt-link">atikrama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duratikrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duratikrama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duratikramaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duratikramā</i> difficile à franchir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durbala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्बल</span> <i>durbala</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-bala" class="skt-link">bala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durbala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durbala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durbalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durbalā</i> faible, chétif; souffreteux, malade, impotent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> frêle, maigre, émacié; précaire.<br> <i>durbalībhū</i> devenir faible ou inefficace.<br> <i>aśvaṃ</i> <i>naiva</i> <i>gajaṃ</i> <i>naiva</i> <i>vyāghraṃ</i> <i>naiva</i> <i>ca</i> <i>naiva</i> <i>ca</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>ajāputraṃ</i> <i>baliṃ</i> <i>dadyād</i> <i>devo</i> <i>durbalaghātakaḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<a href="#entry-Ucaa_nakya" class="skt-link">Cāṇakya</a>] On ne doit sacrifier ni le cheval ni l'éléphant ni surtout le tigre, mais seul le chevreau à Dieu qui tue le faible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durbalaacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>durbalācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] <a href="#entry-durbalaacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe Durbalācārya (début 19⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); on lui doit not. la <a href="#entry-Utattvamiimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">Tattvamīmāṃsā</a>; il est aussi l'auteur de la <a href="#entry-Uku_ncikaa" class="skt-link">Kuñcikā</a>, commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Uvaiyaakara_nasiddhaantama_njuu_saa" class="skt-link">Vaiyākaraṇasiddhāntamañjūṣā</a>; aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_namitraacaarya" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇamitrācārya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-durbala" class="skt-link">durbala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durbhaagya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्भाग्य</span> <i>durbhāgya</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-bhaagya_1" class="skt-link">bhāgya_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durbhaagya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durbhaagya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durbhaagyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durbhāgyā</i> malchanceux; infortuné — <a href="#entry-durbhaagya" class="skt-link">n.</a> infortune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durbhida">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्भिद</span> <i>durbhida</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-bhidaa" class="skt-link">bhidā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durbhida" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durbhida" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durbhidaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durbhidā</i> difficile à détruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durbhik_sam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्भिक्षम्</span> <i>durbhikṣam</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-bhik_saa" class="skt-link">bhikṣā</a>] adv. famine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durdarza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्दर्श</span> <i>durdarśa</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-darza" class="skt-link">darśa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durdarza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durdarza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durdarzaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durdarśā</i> difficile à voir, difficile à rencontrer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dont la vue est pénible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durdazaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्दशा</span> <i>durdaśā</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-dazaa" class="skt-link">daśā</a>] <a href="#entry-durdazaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> infortune, détresse, misère; calamité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durdhar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्धर्ष</span> <i>durdharṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-dhar_sa" class="skt-link">dharṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durdhar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durdhar_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durdhar_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durdharṣā</i> inaccessible, inviolable; dangereux, horrible <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hautain, distant — <a href="#entry-durdhar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Durdharṣa, l'un des 100 fils de <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durdharma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्धर्म</span> <i>durdharma</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durdharma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durdharma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durdharmaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durdharmā</i> de mauvaises mœurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durdhii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्धी</span> <i>durdhī</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-dhii_2" class="skt-link">dhī_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durdhii" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durdhii" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durdhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«mal pensant»] faible d'esprit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mal intentionné, méchant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durdina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्दिन</span> <i>durdina</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-dina" class="skt-link">dina</a>] <a href="#entry-durdina" class="skt-link">n.</a> jour de pluie, jour d'orage; mauvais temps — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(durdināyate)</i> se mettre à pleuvoir, se couvrir (temps).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्ग</span> <i>durga</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durgā</i> d'un accès difficile, inaccessible, impénétrable, inabordable — <a href="#entry-durga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durga" class="skt-link">n.</a> passage difficile, dangereux; forteresse, citadelle, place forte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> difficulté, obstacle, danger, calamité, détresse — f. cf. <a href="#entry-durgaa" class="skt-link">durgā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durgaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्गा</span> <i>durgā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-durga" class="skt-link">durga</a>] <a href="#entry-durgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Durgā «l'Inaccessible», épith. de la Déesse, <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; le lion <a href="#entry-Umanastaala" class="skt-link">Manastāla</a> est sa monture; elle tua l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>-buffle <a href="#entry-Umahi_sa" class="skt-link">Mahiṣa</a> grâce aux armes de tous les dieux réunis; elle tua les frères <a href="#entry-Uzumbha" class="skt-link">Śumbha</a> et <a href="#entry-Unizumbha" class="skt-link">Niśumbha</a> par la colère divine <a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a> sortie de son front; cf. <a href="#entry-Uannapuur_naa" class="skt-link">Annapūrṇā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uambikaa" class="skt-link">Ambikā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uaadyaa" class="skt-link">Ādyā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uiraavatii" class="skt-link">Irāvatī</a>, <a href="#entry-Uiizvarii" class="skt-link">Īśvarī</a>, <a href="#entry-Uumaa" class="skt-link">Umā</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugaurii" class="skt-link">Gaurī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a>, <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dii" class="skt-link">Caṇḍī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ucaamu_n_daa" class="skt-link">Cāmuṇḍā</a>, <a href="#entry-Utripurasundarii" class="skt-link">Tripurasundarī</a>, <a href="#entry-Utvaritaa" class="skt-link">Tvaritā</a>, <a href="#entry-Udazamahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">Daśamahāvidyā</a>, <a href="#entry-Udhuumavatii" class="skt-link">Dhūmavatī</a>, <a href="#entry-Udevii" class="skt-link">Devī</a>, <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubhadrakaalii" class="skt-link">Bhadrakālī</a>, <a href="#entry-Umaatafgii" class="skt-link">Mātaṅgī</a>, <a href="#entry-Umaataa" class="skt-link">Mātā</a>, <a href="#entry-Ulalitaa" class="skt-link">Lalitā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzaakambharii" class="skt-link">Śākaṃbharī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durgaapuujaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>durgāpūjā</i> [<a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a>] <a href="#entry-durgaapuujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-navaraatra" class="skt-link">navarātra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-durgaa" class="skt-link">durgā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durgandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्गन्ध</span> <i>durgandha</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">gandha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durgandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durgandha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durgandhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durgandhā</i> puant, malodorant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durgata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्गत</span> <i>durgata</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-gata" class="skt-link">gata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durgata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durgata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durgataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durgatā</i> pauvre, nécessiteux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durgati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्गति</span> <i>durgati</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-gati" class="skt-link">gati</a>] <a href="#entry-durgati" class="skt-link">f.</a> malheur, misère, détresse, pauvreté; situation difficile <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> manque de ⟨g.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durgha_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्घट</span> <i>durghaṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-gha_ta" class="skt-link">ghaṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durgha_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durgha_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durgha_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durghaṭā</i> difficile à accomplir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durgha_tav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>durghaṭavṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>] <a href="#entry-durgha_tav_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Durghaṭavṛtti «Difficultés morphologiques», œuvre de <a href="#entry-Uzara_nadeva" class="skt-link">Śaraṇadeva</a> discutant d'usages grammaticalement corrects non conformes à <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a> (12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).<br> <a href="#entry-durgha_ta" class="skt-link">durghaṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

  </div>

</div>

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durlabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्लभ</span> <i>durlabha</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-labh" class="skt-link">labh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durlabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durlabha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durlabhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durlabhā</i> inaccessible; introuvable, rare, extraordinaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> difficile à trouver; difficile à ⟨inf.⟩.<br> <i>durlabhībhū</i> devenir inaccessible.<br> <i>durlabham</i> <i>bhārate</i> <i>janma</i> C'est une grande chance que d'être né en Inde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durlabhataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>durlabhatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-durlabhataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> rareté.
  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>durlabhatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-durlabhatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-durlabha" class="skt-link">durlabha</a>
  </div>
</div>

</div>

  </div>
</div>

</div>

</div>

</div>

  </div>

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>
