    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्नामन्</span> <i>durnāman</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-naaman" class="skt-link">nāman</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durnaaman" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durnaaman" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durnaamnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durnāmnī</i> qui a un vilain nom.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durnimitta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्निमित्त</span> <i>durnimitta</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-nimitta" class="skt-link">nimitta</a>] <a href="#entry-durnimitta" class="skt-link">n.</a> mauvais présage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durnivaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्निवार</span> <i>durnivāra</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-nivaara" class="skt-link">nivāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durnivaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durnivaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durnivaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durnivārā</i> difficile à contenir; irrépressible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durnivaarataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>durnivāratā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-durnivaarataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> irrépressibilité.<br> <a href="#entry-durnivaara" class="skt-link">durnivāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुरुक्त</span> <i>durukta</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-ukta" class="skt-link">ukta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duruktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duruktā</i> mal dit, mal prononcé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mauvaise parole, propos blessant ou injurieux, diffamation, mensonge — <a href="#entry-duruktii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duruktī</i> myth. np. de Duruktī, l'Injure personnifiée, fille de <a href="#entry-Ukrodha" class="skt-link">Krodha</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uhi_msaa" class="skt-link">Hiṃsā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duruta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुरुत</span> <i>duruta</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-uta_2" class="skt-link">uta_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duruta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duruta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durutā</i> mal tissé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> au fig. mal élevé, inculte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duruttara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुरुत्तर</span> <i>duruttara</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-uttara_2" class="skt-link">uttara_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duruttara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duruttara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duruttaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>duruttarā</i> difficile à traverser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durv_rtta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्वृत्त</span> <i>durvṛtta</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-v_rtta" class="skt-link">vṛtta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durv_rtta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durv_rtta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durv_rttaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durvṛttā</i> méchant, vicieux — <a href="#entry-durv_rtta" class="skt-link">m.</a> le méchant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durvaacaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्वाचक</span> <i>durvācaka</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-vaacaka" class="skt-link">vācaka</a>] <a href="#entry-durvaacaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> jeu de mots.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durvaacakayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>durvācakayoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] <a href="#entry-durvaacakayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> l'art du jeu de mots, un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-durvaacaka" class="skt-link">durvācaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durvaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्वास</span> <i>durvāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-vaasa_2" class="skt-link">vāsa_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durvaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durvaasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durvaasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durvāsā</i> mal vêtu — <a href="#entry-durvaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> var. <i>durvāsas</i> myth. np. de l'irascible sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Durvāsā «le Mal Vêtu»; il est fils d'<a href="#entry-Uatri" class="skt-link">Atri</a> et d'<a href="#entry-Uanasuuyaa" class="skt-link">Anasūyā</a> comme incarnation de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; sa nature est l'intolérance [<a href="#entry-ak_saanti" class="skt-link">akṣānti</a>]; il est de teint vert et basané, émacié, porte une longue barbe, est vêtu de haillons; il maudit ceux qui l'insultent; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] en récompense pour sa patience et son hospitalité, il donna à <a href="#entry-Ukuntii" class="skt-link">Kuntī</a> un <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> ayant le pouvoir d'invoquer les dieux pour enfanter; il donna à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> le reste de son gruau de riz pour s'en recouvrir le corps comme protection, et le bénit en lui disant qu'il ne pouvait craindre la mort par les parties enduites, mais «qu'il aurait dû s'en couvrir la plante des pieds», prophétisant sa mort par la flèche de <a href="#entry-Ujaraa_h" class="skt-link">Jarāḥ</a>; pour ne pas avoir été reçu avec égards à l'ermitage de <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a>, il provoqua le tragique malentendu entre <a href="#entry-Uzakuntalaa" class="skt-link">Śakuntalā</a> et <a href="#entry-Udu_syanta" class="skt-link">Duṣyanta</a>, en rendant ce dernier amnésique; il exécuta le sacrifice pour <a href="#entry-Uzvetaki" class="skt-link">Śvetaki</a>; il maudit <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> plein d'orgueil de ses richesses pour qu'il perdît la faveur de <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a> qui disparut, pour ne revenir qu'au barattage de l'Océan primordial [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>]; il visita le <a href="#entry-Upit_rloka" class="skt-link">Pitṛloka</a>, et fut surpris des cris de douleur émanant du <a href="#entry-Upit_rtiirtha" class="skt-link">Pitṛtīrtha</a>; <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a> lui apprit que c'était l'enfer où étaient tourmentés les méchants; il demanda à le visiter, et les cendres couvrant son corps le transformèrent en paradis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durvidagdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्विदग्ध</span> <i>durvidagdha</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-vidagdha" class="skt-link">vidagdha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durvidagdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durvidagdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durvidagdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durvidagdhā</i> borné, stupide — <a href="#entry-durvidagdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> faux savant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-durviniita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्विनीत</span> <i>durvinīta</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-viniita" class="skt-link">vinīta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-durviniita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-durviniita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-durviniitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>durvinītā</i> indiscipliné, mal élevé; brutal; rétif — <a href="#entry-durviniita" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Durvinīta «Rétif», qui combattit son frère cadet préféré par leur père <a href="#entry-Uavinita" class="skt-link">Avinita</a> et devint roi de la dynastie Gaṅga du <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_ta" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭa</a> en 529; sa capitale était Talakadu sur la <a href="#entry-Ukaaverii" class="skt-link">Kāverī</a>; une de ses filles épousa <a href="#entry-Upulakezii_2" class="skt-link">Pulakeśī_2</a> et donna naissance à <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya_1" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duryodhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुर्योधन</span> <i>duryodhana</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-yodhana" class="skt-link">yodhana</a>] <a href="#entry-duryodhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Duryodhana «l'Invincible», fils aîné du roi <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ugaandhaarii" class="skt-link">Gāndhārī</a>, général en chef de l'armée des <a href="#entry-kauravaas" class="skt-link">kauravās</a>; il incarne le <a href="#entry-kaliyuga" class="skt-link">kaliyuga</a>; il insulta et maltraita <a href="#entry-Udraupadii" class="skt-link">Draupadī</a>, en la traînant par les cheveux; après la bataille il se cacha dans le lac <a href="#entry-Udvaipaayana_2" class="skt-link">Dvaipāyana_2</a> pour se reposer; il fut tué en combat singulier à la massue par <a href="#entry-Ubhiima" class="skt-link">Bhīma</a>; son fils <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na_1" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa_1</a> fut tué par <a href="#entry-Uabhimanyu" class="skt-link">Abhimanyu</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Usuyodhana" class="skt-link">Suyodhana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dus">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुस्</span> <i>dus</i> var. <i>dur</i>, <i>duś</i>, <i>duṣ</i>, <i>duḥ</i> pf. mal, mauvais, difficile, dangereux, inférieur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. dys.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dustara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुस्तर</span> <i>dustara</i> var. <i>duṣṭarā</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-tara" class="skt-link">tara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dustara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dustara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dustaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dustarā</i> difficile à traverser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dustyaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुस्त्यज</span> <i>dustyaja</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-tyaja" class="skt-link">tyaja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dustyaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dustyaja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dustyajaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dustyajā</i> à quoi il est difficile de renoncer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुत</span> <i>duta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-du" class="skt-link">du</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dutā</i> brûlé, consumé; affligé, désolé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दू</span> <i>dū</i> [<a href="#entry-du" class="skt-link">du</a>] <a href="#entry-duu" class="skt-link">f.</a> douleur; détresse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duu_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दूष्</span> <i>dūṣ</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-du_s" class="skt-link">duṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(dūṣayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dūṣita</i><i>)</i> corrompre, souiller, vicier, gâcher; abîmer, endommager; déshonorer, violer; falsifier, calomnier; insulter, outrager qqn. ⟨g.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> blâmer, désapprouver; objecter, réfuter.<br> <i>svaṃ</i> <i>dūṣayati</i> il se déshonore.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duu_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दूष</span> <i>dūṣa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-duu_s" class="skt-link">dūṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duu_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duu_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duu_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dūṣā</i> qui souille — ifc. souillé de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duu_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दूषण</span> <i>dūṣaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-duu_sa" class="skt-link">dūṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duu_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duu_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duu_sa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dūṣaṇī</i> nuisible, funeste, mauvais; injuste; spoliant — <a href="#entry-duu_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> souillure, péché, faute; outrage, insulte, viol — <a href="#entry-duu_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Dūṣaṇa, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> «le Mauvais» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Dūṣaṇa_1, qui persécuta <a href="#entry-Uvedapriya" class="skt-link">Vedapriya</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-mahaakaalezvara" class="skt-link">mahākāleśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duu_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दूषक</span> <i>dūṣaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-duu_s" class="skt-link">dūṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duu_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duu_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duu_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dūṣikā</i> qui corrompt, qui souille, qui outrage; vicieux, mauvais, impur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> séducteur; diffamateur — <a href="#entry-duu_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dūṣikā</i> impureté, saleté; chassie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duu_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दूषित</span> <i>dūṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-duu_s" class="skt-link">dūṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duu_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duu_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duu_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dūṣitā</i> souillé, corrompu, abîmé; calomnié, outragé — <a href="#entry-duu_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> calomnie, insulte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duuda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dūda</i> [<a href="#entry-da" class="skt-link">da</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duuda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duuda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duudaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dūdā</i> ennemi; oppresseur.<br> <a href="#entry-duu" class="skt-link">dū</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दूर</span> <i>dūra</i> a. <a href="#entry-duura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duuraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dūrā</i> lointain, distant, éloigné — <a href="#entry-duura" class="skt-link">n.</a> éloignement, distance, longue route, grande durée.<br> <i>dūrāt</i> adv. loin, au loin; il y a longtemps.<br> <i>dūram</i> adv. au loin; loin de ⟨abl. g.⟩; très haut ou très bas <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> il y a longtemps <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> énormément.<br> <i>dūratas</i> adv. de loin, à distance; loin; très.<br> <i>dūraṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> distancer, surpasser.<br> <i>dūrīkṛ</i> écarter, éloigner, bannir.<br> <i>dūrībhū</i> s'éloigner, se retirer.<br> <i>dūre</i> <i>kṛ</i> éloigner, séparer.<br> <i>dūre</i> <i>tiṣṭhatu</i> adv. qu'importe, n'en parlons plus.<br> <i>dūre</i> <i>bhū</i> s'éloigner, s'absenter, partir au loin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duurabhaa_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dūrabhāṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_saa" class="skt-link">bhāṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-duurabhaa_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> mod. radio.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duuradarzana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dūradarśana</i> [<a href="#entry-darzana" class="skt-link">darśana</a>] <a href="#entry-duuradarzana" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui voit loin»] vautour — <a href="#entry-duuradarzana" class="skt-link">n.</a> faculté de voir loin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. télévision.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duuradarzin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dūradarśin</i> [<a href="#entry-darzin" class="skt-link">darśin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duuradarzin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duuradarzin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duuradarzinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dūradarśinī</i> qui voit loin — <a href="#entry-duuradarzin" class="skt-link">m.</a> prophète, voyant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vautour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duuravaa_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dūravāṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-vaa_nii" class="skt-link">vāṇī</a>] <a href="#entry-duuravaa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> mod. téléphone.<br> <a href="#entry-duura" class="skt-link">dūra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duurvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दूर्वा</span> <i>dūrvā</i> <a href="#entry-duurvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Cynodon dactylon</i>, panis, chiendent; cette herbe rampante vert sombre est employée dans les rites d'offrande.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duurvaava_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dūrvāvaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-vana" class="skt-link">vana</a>] <a href="#entry-duurvaava_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> véd. bocage d'herbes <a href="#entry-duurvaa" class="skt-link">dūrvā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-duurvaa" class="skt-link">dūrvā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दूत</span> <i>dūta</i> a. <a href="#entry-duuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duutii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dūtī</i> messager, envoyé, émissaire; ambassadeur; négociateur — <a href="#entry-duutii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dūtī</i> entremetteuse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-trika" class="skt-link">trika</a>] partenaire sexuelle d'un <a href="#entry-kulayaaga" class="skt-link">kulayāga</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. attendante de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>.<br> <i>agniṃ</i> <i>dūtam</i> <i>puro</i> <i>dadhe</i> [RV.] Je nomme <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> comme premier ambassadeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duutaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दूतक</span> <i>dūtaka</i> [<a href="#entry-duuta" class="skt-link">dūta</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-duutaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-duutaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-duutikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dūtikā</i> messager; ambassadeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duutakaavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dūtakāvya</i> [<a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>] <a href="#entry-duutakaavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. genre poétique, à l'exemple du <a href="#entry-Umeghaduuta" class="skt-link">Meghadūta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duutavaakya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dūtavākya</i> [<a href="#entry-vaakya" class="skt-link">vākya</a>] <a href="#entry-duutavaakya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Dūtavākya «Le dit du messager», pièce de théâtre du genre <i>kūṭiyāṭṭam</i> du <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duutavaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dūtavāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-vaasa_1" class="skt-link">vāsa_1</a>] <a href="#entry-duutavaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> mod. ambassade.<br> <a href="#entry-duuta" class="skt-link">dūta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-duzce_s_taa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुश्चेष्टा</span> <i>duśceṣṭā</i> [<a href="#entry-dus" class="skt-link">dus</a>-<a href="#entry-ce_s_taa" class="skt-link">ceṣṭā</a>] <a href="#entry-duzce_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> mauvaise conduite, mauvaise action.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्व</span> <i>dva</i> num. <a href="#entry-dva" class="skt-link">*</a> du. deux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. δυο; lat. <i>duo</i>; ang. two; all. zwei; fr. deux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वा</span> <i>dvā</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-dva" class="skt-link">dva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaa_h">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वाः</span> <i>dvāḥ</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-dvaar" class="skt-link">dvār</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaa_hstha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvāḥstha</i> var. <i>dvāstha</i> [<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaa_hstha" class="skt-link">m.</a> portier, garde.<br> <a href="#entry-dvaa_h" class="skt-link">dvāḥ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaa_sa_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvāṣaṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">ṣaṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaa_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> 62; syn. <a href="#entry-dvi_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">dviṣaṣṭi</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-dvaa" class="skt-link">dvā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaacatvaari_mzat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvācatvāriṃśat</i> [<a href="#entry-catvaari_mzat" class="skt-link">catvāriṃśat</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaacatvaari_mzat" class="skt-link">f.</a> 42.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaadaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वादश</span> <i>dvādaśa</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-dvaadazan" class="skt-link">dvādaśan</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaadazaadhyaayii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvādaśādhyāyī</i> [<a href="#entry-adhyaayii" class="skt-link">adhyāyī</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaadazaadhyaayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np de la Dvādaśādhyāyī de <a href="#entry-Ujaimini" class="skt-link">Jaimini</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Udvaadazalak_sa_nii" class="skt-link">Dvādaśalakṣaṇī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaadazaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvādaśānta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaadazaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-trika" class="skt-link">trika</a>] centre psychique [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>] situé à 12 doigts au dessus du <a href="#entry-brahmarandhra" class="skt-link">brahmarandhra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-dvaadaza" class="skt-link">dvādaśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaadazalak_sa_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvādaśalakṣaṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-lak_sa_na" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaadazalak_sa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Dvādaśalakṣaṇī ou <a href="#entry-Udvaadazaadhyaayii" class="skt-link">Dvādaśādhyāyī</a>, formant la <a href="#entry-Umiimaa_msaasuutra" class="skt-link">Mīmāṃsāsūtra</a> «en douze chapitres» de <a href="#entry-Ujaimini" class="skt-link">Jaimini</a>; on l'appelle aussi Tantrakāṇḍa.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaadazan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वादशन्</span> <i>dvādaśan</i> [<a href="#entry-dvaa" class="skt-link">dvā</a>-<a href="#entry-dazan" class="skt-link">daśan</a>] num. <a href="#entry-dvaadazan" class="skt-link">*</a> douze — <a href="#entry-dvaadazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvādaśī</i> astr. 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour d'une quinzaine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. jour de fête du 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire [<a href="#entry-zuklapak_sa" class="skt-link">śuklapakṣa</a>], préparant le festival de pleine lune; il est consacré à la <a href="#entry-tulasii" class="skt-link">tulasī</a>; <a href="#entry-phaalgunii" class="skt-link">phālgunī</a> <a href="#entry-dvaadazii" class="skt-link">dvādaśī</a> inaugure le festival de <a href="#entry-holii" class="skt-link">holī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. jour de fête du 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine sombre [<a href="#entry-k_r_s_napak_sa" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇapakṣa</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-gurudvaadazii" class="skt-link">gurudvādaśī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaadazastotra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvādaśastotra</i> [<a href="#entry-stotra" class="skt-link">stotra</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaadazastotra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Dvādaśastotra, poème dévotionnel en 12 parties de <a href="#entry-Umadhvaacaarya" class="skt-link">Madhvācārya</a> en adoration à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaapara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वापर</span> <i>dvāpara</i> [<a href="#entry-dva" class="skt-link">dva</a>-<a href="#entry-apara" class="skt-link">apara</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaapara" class="skt-link">m.</a> coup de dé de 2 — <a href="#entry-dvaapara" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-dvaaparayuga" class="skt-link">dvāparayuga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaaparayuga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvāparayuga</i> [<a href="#entry-yuga" class="skt-link">yuga</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaaparayuga" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ âge du monde, âge de bronze; son roi en est <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>; [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] les <a href="#entry-vaizyaas" class="skt-link">vaiśyās</a> y acquièrent le droit de pratiquer l'ascèse [<a href="#entry-tapasya" class="skt-link">tapasya</a>]; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] il est personnifié par <a href="#entry-Uzakuni" class="skt-link">Śakuni</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-dvaapara" class="skt-link">dvāpara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वार्</span> <i>dvār</i> <a href="#entry-dvaar" class="skt-link">f.</a> porte, portail — <a href="#entry-dvaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>dvāra</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. θυρα; lat. <i>fores</i>; ang. door; all. Thor.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वार</span> <i>dvāra</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-dvaar" class="skt-link">dvār</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvāraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaaraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> porte, portail — <a href="#entry-dvaarakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvārakā</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de la ville de Dvārakā «aux Grandes portes», capitale du royaume de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> après qu'il fût chassé de <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a> par des démons, à l'ouest du <a href="#entry-Ugurjara" class="skt-link">Gurjara</a>; aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Udvaaravatii" class="skt-link">Dvāravatī</a> et <a href="#entry-Uaanartapura" class="skt-link">Ānartapura</a>; elle fut engloutie par les eaux sept jours après la mort de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>; près d'elle se trouve le siège du <a href="#entry-pazcimaamnaaya" class="skt-link">paścimāmnāya</a> <a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a> fondé par <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>; c'est l'une des 7 villes sacrées [<a href="#entry-saptapurii" class="skt-link">saptapurī</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaarapa_n_dita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvārapaṇḍita</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaarapa_n_dita" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. titre d'érudit «Maître de la porte» au monastère de <a href="#entry-Uvikramaziila" class="skt-link">Vikramaśīla</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaarapaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvārapāla</i> [<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaarapaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> portier, garde <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. <a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a> gardien <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>dvārapālau</i> myth. <a href="#entry-Ujaya_1" class="skt-link">Jaya_1</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvijaya_1" class="skt-link">Vijaya_1</a>, les deux gardes du temple de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; ils en refusèrent un jour l'accès aux quatre éternels toujours jeunes [<a href="#entry-sanakaadi" class="skt-link">sanakādi</a>]; ceux-ci leur jetèrent comme malédiction [<a href="#entry-abhizaapa" class="skt-link">abhiśāpa</a>] de devoir revivre sur Terre; <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> leur donna le choix entre renaître six fois comme ses serviteurs, ou trois fois comme ses ennemis; pour abréger l'épreuve ils choisirent la seconde voie, et revinrent sur Terre, d'abord comme <a href="#entry-Uhira_nyakazipu" class="skt-link">Hiraṇyakaśipu</a> et <a href="#entry-Uhira_nyaak_sa" class="skt-link">Hiraṇyākṣa</a> au <a href="#entry-k_rtayuga" class="skt-link">kṛtayuga</a>, puis comme <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukumbhakar_na" class="skt-link">Kumbhakarṇa</a> au <a href="#entry-tretaayuga" class="skt-link">tretāyuga</a>, enfin comme <a href="#entry-Uzizupaala" class="skt-link">Śiśupāla</a> et <a href="#entry-Udantavakra" class="skt-link">Dantavakra</a> (ou <a href="#entry-Uka_msa" class="skt-link">Kaṃsa</a>) au <a href="#entry-dvaaparayuga" class="skt-link">dvāparayuga</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. <a href="#entry-Unanda" class="skt-link">Nanda</a> et <a href="#entry-Usunanda" class="skt-link">Sunanda</a>, gardes du temple de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.<br> <i>jayaṃ</i> <i>ca</i> <i>vijayaṃ</i> <i>caiva</i> <i>citreṇaiva</i> <i>vinirmitam</i> <i>...</i> On représente les deux gardiens de part et d'autre de l'entrée du sanctuaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaararak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvārarakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-rak_sa" class="skt-link">rakṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaararak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> gardien de porte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaaravat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvāravat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvaaravat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvaaravat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvaaravatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvāravatī</i> muni de nombreuses portes — <a href="#entry-dvaaravatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvāravatī</i> nom de ville capitale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Dvāravatī, capitale de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Udvaarakaa" class="skt-link">Dvārakā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de Dvāravatī_1, capitale de l'empire Mon, vers Nakhon Pathom en mod. Thaïlande (7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ au 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècles).<br> <a href="#entry-dvaara" class="skt-link">dvāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaastha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वास्थ</span> <i>dvāstha</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-dvaar" class="skt-link">dvār</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaastha" class="skt-link">m.</a> porte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaatri_mzikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvātriṃśikā</i> [<a href="#entry-tri_mzikaa" class="skt-link">triṃśikā</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaatri_mzikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> collection de 32 éléments.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वैप</span> <i>dvaipa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvaipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvaipa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvaipaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvaipā</i> relatif à une île, habitant une île.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaipaayana_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvaipāyana_1</i> [<a href="#entry-aayana" class="skt-link">āyana</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaipaayana_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Dvaipāyana_1 «Né sur une île», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a> (né dans une île de la <a href="#entry-Uyamunaa" class="skt-link">Yamunā</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-dvaipa" class="skt-link">dvaipa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaipaayana_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वैपायन</span> <i>dvaipāyana_2</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dvipa" class="skt-link">dvipa</a>-<a href="#entry-ayana" class="skt-link">ayana</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaipaayana_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] nom du lac Dvaipāyana_2 «où les éléphants vont boire»; <a href="#entry-Uduryodhana" class="skt-link">Duryodhana</a> s'y réfugia après avoir perdu la grande bataille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वैत</span> <i>dvaita</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dvi" class="skt-link">dvi</a><a href="#entry--ta" class="skt-link">-ta</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaita" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. dualisme, dualité; doctrine prêchée par <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaitaadvaita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvaitādvaita</i> [<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaitaadvaita" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. dualité dans la non-dualité, doctrine prêchée par <a href="#entry-Unimbaarka" class="skt-link">Nimbārka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-dvaita" class="skt-link">dvaita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaividhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वैविध्य</span> <i>dvaividhya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-dvividha" class="skt-link">dvividha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-dvaividhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> nature double, duplicité; variance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvandva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वन्द्व</span> <i>dvandva</i> var. <i>dvaṃdva</i> [red. <a href="#entry-dva" class="skt-link">dva</a>] <a href="#entry-dvandva" class="skt-link">n.</a> paire, couple; dualité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> querelle, combat, duel; alternative, dilemme — <a href="#entry-dvandva" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. mot composé copulatif (au duel); not. paire de notions opposées.<br> <i>dvandvam</i> var. <i>dvandvena</i> adv. par couple, deux à deux, face à face.<br> <i>sāmānyabhūmipau</i> du. (composé copulatif) le commun et le roi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvandvamoha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvandvamoha</i> [<a href="#entry-moha" class="skt-link">moha</a>] <a href="#entry-dvandvamoha" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. illusion des dualités.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvandvavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvandvavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-dvandvavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> dialectique.<br> <a href="#entry-dvandva" class="skt-link">dvandva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वय</span> <i>dvaya</i> [<a href="#entry-dva" class="skt-link">dva</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvayī</i> double; de deux sortes — <a href="#entry-dvaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> paire, couple — ifc. les deux, la paire de — <a href="#entry-dvayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvayā</i> ifc. id.<br> <i>dvayam</i> adv. doublement.<br> <i>daladvaya</i> n. les deux morceaux (sg.).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvayos">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वयोस्</span> <i>dvayos</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-dva" class="skt-link">dva</a>] ind. gram. se dit d'un mot utilisable avec les genres m. et f.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dve_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वेष</span> <i>dveṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-dvi_s_1" class="skt-link">dviṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-dve_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> haine; dégoût, aversion (opp. <a href="#entry-icchaa" class="skt-link">icchā</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>] la propriété [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] d'aversion — ifc. antipathie pour, aversion pour.<br> <i>dveṣaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> manifester son aversion envers ⟨dat.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dve_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वेषिन्</span> <i>dveṣin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dvi_s_1" class="skt-link">dviṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dve_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dve_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dve_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dveṣiṇī</i> ennemi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dve_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वेष्य</span> <i>dveṣya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-dvi_s_1" class="skt-link">dviṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dve_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dve_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dve_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dveṣyā</i> à abhorrer, à haïr; odieux, détestable — <a href="#entry-dve_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> ennemi — <a href="#entry-dve_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> noix de muscade.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dve_syataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dveṣyatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-dve_syataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> fait d'être odieux; défaveur, rejet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dve_syatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dveṣyatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-dve_syatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'être odieux; défaveur, rejet.<br> <a href="#entry-dve_sya" class="skt-link">dveṣya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वि</span> <i>dvi</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-dva" class="skt-link">dva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvi_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्विष्ट</span> <i>dviṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-dvi_s_1" class="skt-link">dviṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvi_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvi_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvi_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dviṣṭā</i> haï, détesté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> odieux, hostile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvi_s_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्विष्</span> <i>dviṣ_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(dveṣṭi)</i> pr. md. <i>(dviṣṭe)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>dviṣṭa</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(dviṣat)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> haïr, détester, traiter en ennemi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvi_s_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्विष्</span> <i>dviṣ_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dvi_s_1" class="skt-link">dviṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-dvi_s_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> ennemi — ifc. qui hait, hostile à, ennemi de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>dviṣa</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvi_sa_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dviṣaṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">ṣaṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-dvi_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> 62; syn. <a href="#entry-dvaa_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">dvāṣaṣṭi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvi_sat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्विषत्</span> <i>dviṣat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-dvi_s_1" class="skt-link">dviṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvi_sat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvi_sat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvi_satii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dviṣatī</i> haineux; hostile à, ennemi de ⟨acc. g.⟩ — <a href="#entry-dvi_sat" class="skt-link">m.</a> ennemi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvibhuja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvibhuja</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuja" class="skt-link">bhuja</a>] <a href="#entry-dvibhuja" class="skt-link">m.</a> math. angle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvicatvaari_mzat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvicatvāriṃśat</i> [<a href="#entry-catvaari_mzat" class="skt-link">catvāriṃśat</a>] <a href="#entry-dvicatvaari_mzat" class="skt-link">f.</a> 42.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvida_n_din">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvidaṇḍin</i> [<a href="#entry-da_n_din" class="skt-link">daṇḍin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvida_n_din" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvida_n_din" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvida_n_dinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvidaṇḍinī</i> qui porte deux bâtons (se dit de mendiants).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvidhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्विधा</span> <i>dvidhā</i> [<a href="#entry-dvi" class="skt-link">dvi</a><a href="#entry--dhaa" class="skt-link">-dhā</a>] adv. doublement, 2 fois; en 2 parties; de 2 manières.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvigu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvigu</i> [<a href="#entry-gu" class="skt-link">gu</a>] <a href="#entry-dvigu" class="skt-link">m.</a> (qui vaut) deux vaches <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. [générique] catégorie de composés dont le premier terme est un nombre (comme <a href="#entry-dvigu" class="skt-link">dvigu</a>).<br> <i>dvigur</i> <i>api</i> <i>sadvandvo'ham</i> <i>gṛhe</i> <i>ca</i> <i>me</i> <i>satatam</i> <i>avyayībhāvaḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>tat</i> <i>puruṣa</i> <i>karma</i> <i>dhāraya</i> <i>yenāhaṃ</i> <i>syāṃ</i> <i>bahuvrīhiḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (mnémotechnique des sortes de composés) Bien que j'aie deux vaches, je suis querelleur, et chez moi on ne paye pas les factures; ainsi, Ô homme, travaille dur que je puisse devenir riche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvihaayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvihāyana</i> [<a href="#entry-haayana" class="skt-link">hāyana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvihaayana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvihaayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvihaayanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvihāyanī</i> de deux ans d'âge — <a href="#entry-dvihaayanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvihāyanī</i> vache de deux ans.<br> <a href="#entry-dvi" class="skt-link">dvi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dviipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वीप</span> <i>dvīpa</i> <a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">n.</a> île <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. continent; la géographie mythique en dénombre 7: au centre le <a href="#entry-jambu" class="skt-link">jambu</a>, puis le <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a>, le <a href="#entry-plak_sa" class="skt-link">plakṣa</a>, le <a href="#entry-zaalmali" class="skt-link">śālmali</a>, le <a href="#entry-krau_nca" class="skt-link">krauñca</a>, le <a href="#entry-zaaka_1" class="skt-link">śāka_1</a> et le <a href="#entry-pu_skara" class="skt-link">puṣkara</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> l'une des 10 régions formant le <a href="#entry-Ujambudviipa" class="skt-link">Jambudvīpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dviipin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वीपिन्</span> <i>dvīpin</i> [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dviipin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dviipin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dviipinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvīpinī</i> tacheté [«qui a des îles (sur la peau ou la fourrure)»] — <a href="#entry-dviipin" class="skt-link">m.</a> léopard, panthère, tigre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvija</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvija" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvija" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvijaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvijā</i> deux fois né — <a href="#entry-dvija" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. homme aryen, de l'une des 3 premières classes [<a href="#entry-var_na" class="skt-link">varṇa</a>], ayant accompli l'<a href="#entry-upanayana" class="skt-link">upanayana</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-brahmajanman" class="skt-link">brahmajanman</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> oiseau, animal ovipare en gén. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. la Lune; cf. <a href="#entry-Uatri" class="skt-link">Atri</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvijaanu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvijānu</i> [<a href="#entry-jaanu" class="skt-link">jānu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvijaanu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvijaanu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvijaanu" class="skt-link">f.</a> (de l'eau) jusqu'aux deux genoux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvijaati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvijāti</i> [<a href="#entry-jaati" class="skt-link">jāti</a>] <a href="#entry-dvijaati" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme aryen.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvijihva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvijihva</i> [<a href="#entry-jihvaa" class="skt-link">jihvā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvijihva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvijihva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvijihvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvijihvā</i> [«qui a deux langues»] menteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvijottama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvijottama</i> [<a href="#entry-uttama" class="skt-link">uttama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvijottama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvijottama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvijottamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvijottamā</i> super. le meilleur des brâhmanes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. prêtre principal.<br> <a href="#entry-dvija" class="skt-link">dvija</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvikara_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvikaraṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_nii" class="skt-link">karaṇī</a>] <a href="#entry-dvikara_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> math. diagonale d'un carré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvikarmaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvikarmaka</i> [<a href="#entry-karmaka" class="skt-link">karmaka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvikarmaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvikarmaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvikarmikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvikarmikā</i> gram. se dit d'un verbe admettant deux compléments à l'accusatif; l'un est dit principal [<a href="#entry-mukhya" class="skt-link">mukhya</a>], l'autre secondaire [<a href="#entry-gau_na" class="skt-link">gauṇa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvikuu_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvikūṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-kuu_ta" class="skt-link">kūṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvikuu_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvikuu_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvikuu_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvikūṭā</i> arch. se dit d'un temple à deux sanctuaires.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvimii_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvimīḍha</i> [<a href="#entry-mii_dha" class="skt-link">mīḍha</a>] <a href="#entry-dvimii_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Dvimīḍha, deuxième fils de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhatputra" class="skt-link">Bṛhatputra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvimuni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvimuni</i> [<a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a>] ind. gram. (n.) les deux Sages de la tradition grammaticale sanskrite (<a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a> et <a href="#entry-Upata_njali_1" class="skt-link">Patañjali_1</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvipa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_1" class="skt-link">pa_1</a>] <a href="#entry-dvipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> éléphant [«qui boit en 2 fois»] (avec la trompe et la bouche).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvipad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvipad</i> [<a href="#entry-pad_2" class="skt-link">pad_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvipad" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvipad" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvipad" class="skt-link">f.</a> bipède.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvipada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvipada</i> [<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvipada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvipada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvipadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvipadā</i> gram. consistant en 2 mots; contenant 2 mots <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> de deux pieds de long (brique) — <a href="#entry-dvipada" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«bestiole à deux pattes»] (dérogatoire) homme — <a href="#entry-dvipadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvipadā</i> strophe de deux pieds — <a href="#entry-dvipadii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvipadī</i> phon. mètre <a href="#entry-prak_rta" class="skt-link">prakṛta</a>; vers dans ce mètre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvipari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvipari</i> [<a href="#entry-pari" class="skt-link">pari</a>] ind. manqué de deux (not. au jeu <a href="#entry-pa_ncaka" class="skt-link">pañcaka</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvipendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvipendra</i> [<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-dvipendra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Dvipendra, épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uairaavata" class="skt-link">Airāvata</a> «Roi des éléphants».<br> <a href="#entry-dvipa" class="skt-link">dvipa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dviprahara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dviprahara</i> [<a href="#entry-prahara" class="skt-link">prahara</a>] <a href="#entry-dviprahara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. garde matinale (de 8 h à 11 h).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvir">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्विर्</span> <i>dvir</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-dvis" class="skt-link">dvis</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvirepha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvirepha</i> [<a href="#entry-repha" class="skt-link">repha</a>] <a href="#entry-dvirepha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. [«en forme de 2 ‘r’»] gros hyménoptère noir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvirukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvirukta</i> [<a href="#entry-ukta" class="skt-link">ukta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvirukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvirukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dviruktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dviruktā</i> répété.<br> <a href="#entry-dvir" class="skt-link">dvir</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvis">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्विस्</span> <i>dvis</i> [<a href="#entry-dvi" class="skt-link">dvi</a>] adv. deux fois, à deux reprises <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. twice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvisandhaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvisandhāna</i> [<a href="#entry-sandhaana" class="skt-link">sandhāna</a>] <a href="#entry-dvisandhaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> amalgamation de deux composés <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. procédé poétique à double but, inventé par <a href="#entry-Udhana_njaya_1" class="skt-link">Dhanañjaya_1</a> et théorisé par <a href="#entry-Ubhoja_1" class="skt-link">Bhoja_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvisandhaanakaavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvisandhānakāvya</i> [<a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>] <a href="#entry-dvisandhaanakaavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Dvisandhānakāvya ou Dvisandhāna <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> de <a href="#entry-Udhana_njaya_1" class="skt-link">Dhanañjaya_1</a>, combinant le <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a> et le <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a> grâce au procédé de coalescence [<a href="#entry-zle_sa" class="skt-link">śleṣa</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-dvyarthakaavya" class="skt-link">dvyarthakāvya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-dvisandhaana" class="skt-link">dvisandhāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvisandhaanakavi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvisandhānakavi</i> [<a href="#entry-kavi" class="skt-link">kavi</a>] <a href="#entry-dvisandhaanakavi" class="skt-link">m.</a> «poète de la double narration», épith. de <a href="#entry-Udhana_njaya_1" class="skt-link">Dhanañjaya_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvisandhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvisandhi</i> [<a href="#entry-sandhi" class="skt-link">sandhi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvisandhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvisandhi" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvisandhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> composé de deux parties <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. qui admet un <a href="#entry-sandhi" class="skt-link">sandhi</a> ambigu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वित</span> <i>dvita</i> [<a href="#entry-dvi" class="skt-link">dvi</a><a href="#entry--ta" class="skt-link">-ta</a>] <a href="#entry-dvita" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Dvita «Second», fils de <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a>, frère indigne de <a href="#entry-Utrita" class="skt-link">Trita</a>; il fut transformé en singe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्विता</span> <i>dvitā</i> [<a href="#entry-dvi" class="skt-link">dvi</a><a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-dvitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> fait d'être double; dualité — adv. raison de plus; justement; certainement, spécialement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvitaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वितय</span> <i>dvitaya</i> [<a href="#entry-dvi" class="skt-link">dvi</a><a href="#entry--taya" class="skt-link">-taya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvitaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvitaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvitayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvitayī</i> double, constitué de deux parties; les deux — <a href="#entry-dvitaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> paire, couple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvitiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्वितीय</span> <i>dvitīya</i> [<a href="#entry-dvi" class="skt-link">dvi</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvitiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvitiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvitiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvitīyā</i> ord. deuxième, second <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. (mois) intercalaire — ifc. ayant pour second, accompagné de, muni de — <a href="#entry-dvitiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> deuxième fils de la famille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. deuxième phonème d'un <a href="#entry-varga" class="skt-link">varga</a> (consonne sourde aspirée) — <a href="#entry-dvitiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> ifc. moitié de — <a href="#entry-dvitiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvitīyā</i> accompagnante, amie proche; épouse (autre soi) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. accusatif (2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ déclinaison) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. deuxième <a href="#entry-tithi" class="skt-link">tithi</a> d'un <a href="#entry-pak_sa" class="skt-link">pakṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvitiiyaraajatarafgi_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvitīyarājataraṅgiṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-raajatarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">rājataraṅgiṇī</a>] <a href="#entry-dvitiiyaraajatarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de l'épopée dynastique du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> Dvitīyarājataraṅgiṇī de <a href="#entry-Ujonaraaja" class="skt-link">Jonarāja</a> (1412), aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Uraajaavali" class="skt-link">Rājāvali</a>; elle fait suite à la <a href="#entry-Uraajatarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">Rājataraṅgiṇī</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukalha_na" class="skt-link">Kalhaṇa</a>; elle fut continuée par son élève <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a> <a href="#entry-Uvara" class="skt-link">Vara</a> (1477).<br> <a href="#entry-dvitiiya" class="skt-link">dvitīya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvitra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvitra</i> [<a href="#entry-tri" class="skt-link">tri</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvitra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvitra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvitraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvitrā</i> pl. deux ou trois.
  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvivacana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dviveda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dviveda</i> [<a href="#entry-veda" class="skt-link">veda</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dviveda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dviveda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvivedii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvivedī</i> qui maîtrise 2 Védas.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvivedin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvivedin</i> [<a href="#entry-vedin" class="skt-link">vedin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvivedin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvivedin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvivedinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvivedinī</i> qui maîtrise 2 Védas.

  </div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvizata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dviśata</i> [<a href="#entry-zata" class="skt-link">śata</a>] <a href="#entry-dvizata" class="skt-link">n.</a> 200 — <a href="#entry-dvizatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dviśatī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvyaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्व्यह</span> <i>dvyaha</i> [<a href="#entry-dvi" class="skt-link">dvi</a>-<a href="#entry-aha" class="skt-link">aha</a>] <a href="#entry-dvyaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> période de 2 jours — a. <a href="#entry-dvyaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvyaha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvyahaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvyahā</i> qui dure 2 jours — ind. pendant 2 jours; après 2 jours.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvyahaatyaasam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvyahātyāsam</i> [<a href="#entry-atyaasam" class="skt-link">atyāsam</a>] ind. deux jours plus tard <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> every 3 days.<br> <a href="#entry-dvyaha" class="skt-link">dvyaha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvyahatar_sam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dvyahatarṣam</i> [<a href="#entry-tar_sam" class="skt-link">tarṣam</a>] ind. ayant assoiffé pendant 2 jours.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dvyartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्व्यर्थ</span> <i>dvyartha</i> [<a href="#entry-dvi" class="skt-link">dvi</a>-<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dvyartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dvyartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dvyarthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dvyarthā</i> à double sens.
  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyaavaap_rthivii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyaus">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्यौस्</span> <i>dyaus</i> [nom. <a href="#entry-div" class="skt-link">div</a>] ind. myth. np. de Dyau, le Ciel-Lumière personnifié, un trésor [<a href="#entry-vasu" class="skt-link">vasu</a>]; <a href="#entry-Up_rthivii" class="skt-link">Pṛthivī</a> est son épouse; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] pour le punir d'avoir volé sa vache <a href="#entry-Unandinii" class="skt-link">Nandinī</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> le condamna à s'incarner sur Terre comme <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugafgaa" class="skt-link">Gaṅgā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyot">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyota">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्योत</span> <i>dyota</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dyut_1" class="skt-link">dyut_1</a>] <a href="#entry-dyota" class="skt-link">m.</a> lumière, luminosité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyotaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्योतक</span> <i>dyotaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dyut_1" class="skt-link">dyut_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dyotaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dyotaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dyotikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dyotikā</i> qui éclaire, qui illumine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. mot grammatical; opp. <a href="#entry-vaacaka" class="skt-link">vācaka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> explication; sens — ifc. qui éclaircit, qui explique.
  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dyotakatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-dyotakatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. théorie du sens de <a href="#entry-Upata_njali_1" class="skt-link">Patañjali_1</a>; elle explique la formation morphologique par composition sémantique.<br> <a href="#entry-dyotaka" class="skt-link">dyotaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyotita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyotya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्योत्य</span> <i>dyotya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-dyot" class="skt-link">dyot</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dyotya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dyotya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dyotyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dyotyā</i> à expliquer; qui doit être exprimé ou suggéré — <a href="#entry-dyotya" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. connotation; sous-entendu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyukaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyumat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyumna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्युम्न</span> <i>dyumna</i> [<a href="#entry-dyumat" class="skt-link">dyumat</a>] <a href="#entry-dyumna" class="skt-link">n.</a> splendeur, éclat; majesté, gloire; vigueur; fortune, richesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyut_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्युत्</span> <i>dyut_1</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-diiv_1" class="skt-link">dīv_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(dyotate)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <i>(adyutat)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(adidyutat)</i> ppr. <i>(dyumat)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> briller, resplendir — ca. <i>(dyotayati)</i> illuminer, irradier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> expliquer, élucider, rendre lumineux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyut_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्युत्</span> <i>dyut_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-dyut_1" class="skt-link">dyut_1</a>] <a href="#entry-dyut_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> éclat, rayon de lumière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyuti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्युति</span> <i>dyuti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-dyut_1" class="skt-link">dyut_1</a>] <a href="#entry-dyuti" class="skt-link">f.</a> éclat, splendeur; majesté, gloire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyutimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dyutimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dyutimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dyutimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dyutimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dyutimatī</i> resplendissant.<br> <a href="#entry-dyuti" class="skt-link">dyuti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyuuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">द्यूत</span> <i>dyūta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-diiv_1" class="skt-link">dīv_1</a>] <a href="#entry-dyuuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> jeu de dés; pari, jeu de hasard; compétition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gain au jeu, butin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyuutakuzala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dyūtakuśala</i> [<a href="#entry-kuzala" class="skt-link">kuśala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-dyuutakuzala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-dyuutakuzala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-dyuutakuzalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dyūtakuśalā</i> doué au jeu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyuutama_n_dala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>dyūtamaṇḍala</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_n_dala" class="skt-link">maṇḍala</a>] <a href="#entry-dyuutama_n_dala" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. cercle de jeu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyuutaparvan">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-dyuutavize_sa">

  </div>
</div>
